{"parentUuid":"d6b227f3-fb43-4f34-a0e9-fa4ad285490c","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"96303632-9da0-49ca-b24e-0e1d26baa801","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UmySCbs9eJwrpuFS61Rjxa","type":"tool_result","content":"## Problem Statement\n\nThe Golf & Country Club of Trujillo announces news, events and the weekly restaurant menu through WhatsApp groups and Instagram. Messages get buried, they're hard to search, and there's no single place that's always up to date. Members keep asking the office the same questions.\n\nThe office wants to post an update in a few minutes without asking anyone for help. The club has three fears:\n- a staff member publishes an unfinished post, or a wrong menu or price, by mistake;\n- someone who should only post news changes the rules or membership Pages, or adds or removes Users;\n- a Draft shows up on the public site.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS with two halves, following ADR-001 and ADR-002.\n\n- **Admin console** (English, local only). Editors write and publish Posts of three kinds: News, Event and Menu. Admins also manage Pages (rules, membership, about) and Users. Every change Members will see needs an explicit Confirmation, with a preview.\n- **Public site** (Spanish, static). Each Export renders Published content into `site/`, and it is deployed to GitHub Pages. The generated Home page shows the Current menu, Upcoming Events and the latest News. Navigation adds three fixed feeds (News, Events, Menu archive) and the Admin's Pages in the order the Admin sets.\n\nThe developer runs the Export, never the office. The console shows how many changes are not yet Live, so nobody confuses Published with Live.\n\nVocabulary follows `CONTEXT.md` throughout.\n\n## User Stories\n\n### Login and session\n\n1. As a User, I want to sign in with my email and password, so that only club staff can reach the console.\n2. As a User, I want a wrong email or password to give the same generic error, so that nobody can find out which emails have accounts.\n3. As a User, I want to sign out, so that the next person at the office computer isn't working as me.\n4. As a Deactivated User, I want my login to be refused, so that former staff can't change the site.\n5. As an Admin, I want a User I deactivate to lose access on their very next request, even if they're already signed in, so that deactivation takes effect immediately.\n6. As a visitor who isn't signed in, I want every console address to send me to the login screen, so that no console content leaks.\n7. As the club, I want passwords stored only as argon2 hashes and never logged, so that a copied database doesn't reveal them.\n8. As the club, I want every form that changes something to carry a CSRF token, so that another website can't make a signed-in User's browser change content.\n\n### Roles\n\n9. As an Editor, I want the console to show me only Posts, the dashboard and signing out, so that I'm not distracted by things I can't use.\n10. As the club, I want an Editor who types a Page or User address directly to be refused with a \"not allowed\" response, so that the restriction is real and not just a hidden link.\n11. As an Admin, I want to do everything an Editor can, plus manage Pages and Users, so that one person can run the whole site.\n\n### Posts\n\n12. As an Editor, I want to create a Post by choosing its kind (News, Event or Menu), so that it appears in the right place on the public site.\n13. As an Editor, I want to write a Post body in Markdown, so that I can add headings, lists and links without a complicated editor.\n14. As an Editor, I want a new Post to start as a Draft, so that nothing reaches Members until I decide.\n15. As an Editor, I want the Slug generated from the title with accents stripped (\"Menú de la semana\" → `menu-de-la-semana`), so that I never have to think about addresses.\n16. As an Editor, I want to edit the Slug before first publish, so that I can shorten an awkward one.\n17. As a Member, I want a Slug to never change once content has been Published, even after it's Unpublished and republished, so that links shared on WhatsApp keep working.\n18. As an Editor, I want a clash with an existing Slug to be fixed automatically by adding a number (`-2`, `-3`), so that I can reuse a common title such as \"Menú de la semana\" every week.\n19. As an Editor, I want a clear error if I type a Slug that's already taken, so that I don't overwrite someone else's address.\n20. As an Editor, I want to preview a Post as Members will see it (Spanish public layout, sanitized Markdown), so that I catch mistakes before they go Live.\n21. As an Editor, I want to edit, publish, unpublish or delete any Post, including one written by another Editor, so that whoever is in the office can fix Friday's menu on Monday.\n22. As the club, I want the Author to stay the original writer when someone else edits a Post, so that the console records who created it.\n23. As an Admin, I want to do anything to Posts that an Editor can, so that I can cover for the office.\n\n### Events\n\n24. As an Editor, I want to give an Event a start date, an optional start time and an optional end date, so that a dinner at 8 pm and a Saturday–Sunday tournament are both described precisely.\n25. As an Editor, I want an Event without a start date to be rejected, so that every Event can be sorted.\n26. As an Editor, I want an end date earlier than the start date to be rejected with a clear message, so that impossible ranges never go Live.\n27. As a Member, I want the Event date shown first and prominently on every Event, so that I can tell at a glance when it is.\n28. As a Member, I want Upcoming Events listed soonest first, so that \"when is the next event?\" is answered at the top.\n29. As a Member, I want a weekend tournament to stay Upcoming until its end date has passed, so that I can still find it while it's happening.\n\n### Menu\n\n30. As an Editor, I want to post each week's menu as a new Menu Post, so that updating it takes a few minutes.\n31. As a Member, I want the Home page to show only the Current menu, so that I never read last week's prices by mistake.\n32. As a Member, I want older Menus in a Menu archive, so that I can look up what was served before.\n33. As an Editor, I want the previous Menu to leave the Home page automatically when the new one goes Live, so that nobody has to remember to unpublish it.\n\n### Confirmation\n\n34. As an Editor, I want publishing to go through a Confirmation screen showing the preview, so that an unfinished post or a wrong price isn't published by one careless click.\n35. As an Editor, I want saving edits to Published content to go through the same Confirmation, so that a price change to a Live menu is checked before it goes out.\n36. As an Editor, I want unpublishing to need Confirmation, so that I don't accidentally take down something Members rely on.\n37. As an Editor, I want deleting to need Confirmation that says it's permanent, so that I don't lose content by mistake.\n38. As an Editor, I want to cancel on the Confirmation screen and return to my unsaved edits, so that I can fix the mistake I just noticed.\n39. As an Editor, I want saving a Draft to need no Confirmation, so that everyday writing stays quick.\n\n### Pages\n\n40. As an Admin, I want to create, edit, preview, publish, unpublish and delete Pages (rules, membership, about) with the same Draft/Published lifecycle and Confirmation as Posts, so that I only learn one workflow.\n41. As an Admin, I want to give each Page a navigation order number, so that Members see the Pages in a sensible order.\n42. As a Member, I want every Live Page to appear in the Navigation, so that I can find the rules and membership information.\n43. As the club, I want a Page Slug that clashes with a public-site address (`index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`) to be rejected, so that a Page can never overwrite the Home page or a feed.\n\n### Users\n\n44. As an Admin, I want to create a User with a name, email, role and initial password, so that new office staff can start posting.\n45. As an Admin, I want to change a User's role between Admin and Editor, so that responsibilities can change.\n46. As an Admin, I want to deactivate and reactivate a User, so that former staff lose access without their content or authorship being lost.\n47. As an Admin, I want to be prevented from deactivating or demoting myself, so that the club always has an Admin.\n48. As an Admin, I want to see all Users with their role and whether they're active, so that I know who has access.\n49. As the club, I want Users never to be deleted, so that every Post and Page keeps its Author.\n\n### Dashboard and content lists\n\n50. As a User, I want a dashboard with counts of Posts by kind and status, and (for Admins) Pages by status, so that I can see the state of the site at a glance.\n51. As a User, I want the dashboard to show how many changes are not yet Live and when the last Export happened, so that I know when to ask for an Export.\n52. As a User, I want to filter the Post list by kind and by status, so that I can find this week's Draft menu quickly.\n53. As a User, I want the Post list to show title, kind, status, Author and last-updated time in Lima time, so that I can tell what changed recently.\n54. As a User, I want status filters that match no content to still appear, showing zero, so that the list doesn't change shape the way WordPress's did.\n\n### Public site\n\n55. As a Member, I want a Home page with the Current menu, the next Upcoming Events and the latest News, so that I can find what I need in a few seconds.\n56. As a Member, I want News, Events and Menu archive feeds in the Navigation, so that I can browse each kind of Post.\n57. As a Member, I want the Events feed to show Upcoming Events first and then past Events, most recent first, so that the list stays useful after an event is over.\n58. As a Member, I want the public site entirely in Spanish, dates included (\"29 de septiembre de 2026\"), so that it reads naturally.\n59. As a Member, I want every date and time shown in Lima time, so that a post never appears dated tomorrow.\n60. As a Member, I want News and Menus dated by when they were first published, so that the date means \"when the club said this\".\n61. As the club, I want no Author names on the public site, so that the club speaks as one voice.\n62. As the club, I want Drafts never to appear on the public site in any form (page, feed entry, Home page or Navigation), so that the club's third fear can't happen.\n63. As the club, I want user-written Markdown sanitized before it's rendered, so that a pasted `<script>` can't run in a Member's browser.\n64. As an Editor, I want to embed an image hosted elsewhere (for example on the club's Instagram) through a Markdown image link, so that posts can have pictures without uploads.\n65. As a Member, I want the site to work from its GitHub Pages subfolder, so that every link and stylesheet loads.\n\n### Export\n\n66. As the developer, I want an Export to render only Published content into `site/` and deploy it, so that what Members see is exactly what is Published.\n67. As the developer, I want each Export to decide Upcoming from the export date in Lima time, so that the result is predictable.\n68. As the developer, I want each Export to record when it ran, so that the console can count changes that aren't Live yet.\n69. As a grader, I want the seed script to create an Admin, an Editor and demo content (at least one Draft and one Published item of each kind, including a multi-day Event), so that the CMS can be tried right away from a fresh clone.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n### Modules\n\n- **Accounts:** Users, argon2 hashing, login and logout, the signed session cookie, and the rule that a Deactivated User is refused both at login and on every request.\n- **Permissions:** the role check shared by all console routes. Editor: Posts and the dashboard. Admin: everything. It's enforced server-side, and a refusal returns 403.\n- **CSRF:** a per-session token, a hidden field in every state-changing form, and a check on every non-GET console request.\n- **Content:** Posts and Pages, their Draft/Published lifecycle, Slug generation, clash handling and locking, Event-date validation, and the queries for Current menu, Upcoming and the feeds. The console and the Export share it, so both use the same definitions.\n- **Markdown rendering:** one function, used by both Preview and Export, that turns Markdown into sanitized HTML (markdown-it, then nh3). It allows `img` only with an `http(s)` source.\n- **Console routes and templates:** login, dashboard, Post list and editor, Page list and editor, Users, and Confirmation screens. The console is in English.\n- **Export:** `render_site` reads the database and writes the Spanish public site. The `cms publish` and `cms deploy` commands together make up an Export.\n- **Time:** helpers for UTC storage, America/Lima display (standard-library `zoneinfo`) and Spanish date formatting from a hard-coded month list. No new dependency.\n\n### Schema (SQLite; no ORM, following ADR-002's stack)\n\n- **users:** id, name, email (unique, case-insensitive), password_hash, role (`admin` | `editor`), active, created_at.\n- **posts:** id, kind (`news` | `event` | `menu`, fixed at creation), title, slug, body_md, status (`draft` | `published`), author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at, and for Events only: event_start_date, event_start_time (nullable), event_end_date (nullable). The slug is unique within a kind.\n- **pages:** id, title, slug (unique), body_md, status, nav_order, author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at.\n- **exports:** the time each Export ran, plus a count of Published items deleted since then, used by the not-yet-Live indicator.\n- All timestamps are stored in UTC.\n\n### Rules\n\n- **Slug:** generated from the title, lower-case ASCII with accents stripped and hyphens between words. An auto-generated clash gets a numeric suffix; a slug typed by hand that clashes is a form error. The slug can be edited only while `first_published_at` is empty, and once set, that field is never cleared, even by Unpublishing.\n- **Reserved Page Slugs:** `index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`.\n- **Event date:** a start date is required. The end date must be on or after the start date. Event fields are rejected on News and Menu Posts.\n- **Upcoming:** `coalesce(event_end_date, event_start_date) >= export_date`, where the export date is a date in America/Lima. The Home page shows the next 3 Upcoming Events.\n- **Current menu:** the Published Menu with the latest `first_published_at`. Every other Published Menu goes to the Menu archive, newest first.\n- **Home page:** the Current menu, the next 3 Upcoming Events and the latest 5 News, all on one page.\n- **Confirmation (no JavaScript):** submitting a change that Members will see (publish, unpublish, delete, or saving Published content) returns a Confirmation page. That page shows a summary and, where relevant, the rendered preview, and carries the pending values in hidden fields with the CSRF token. Only a second POST that includes the confirm flag applies the change. Cancel returns to the editor with the values intact. Saving a Draft applies immediately.\n- **Self-protection:** a request that would deactivate the Admin making it, or change that Admin's own role, is refused with an explanation.\n- **Not-yet-Live indicator:** Posts and Pages whose `updated_at` is after the last Export, plus Published items deleted since then. If there has never been an Export, every Published item counts.\n- **First Admin:** the seed script creates the first Admin and Editor, taking their passwords from `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` and `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD`. No separate bootstrap command.\n\n### Public site layout (all links relative)\n\n- `index.html`: the Home page.\n- `noticias/index.html` and `noticias/<slug>.html`: News.\n- `eventos/index.html` and `eventos/<slug>.html`: Events (Upcoming, then past).\n- `menus/index.html` and `menus/<slug>.html`: the Menu archive and each Menu.\n- `<slug>.html`: Pages.\n- `style.css`.\n- Navigation order: Inicio, Noticias, Eventos, Menús anteriores, then Pages by nav_order (ties broken by title).\n- The site title comes from `CMS_SITE_TITLE`, set to the club's name in `.env.example`.\n\n### Interfaces that change\n\n- `create_app()` accepts a database path (default from settings), so tests can give each app its own database.\n- `render_site(out, export_date=None)` reads Published content from the configured database. `export_date` defaults to today in America/Lima and records the Export.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **A good test** exercises behaviour through a public seam and asserts only what a User or Member could observe: status codes, redirects, rendered console HTML, and the files and HTML written to `site/`. Tests never query tables or call internal helpers. That lets the storage and module layout change without rewriting tests.\n- **Seam 1, the console over HTTP:** `create_app()` driven by the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures. This covers login and deactivation, Permissions (Editor gets 403 on Pages and Users), CSRF rejection, the Post and Page lifecycle, Slug rules, Event-date validation, Confirmation (the first POST changes nothing; the confirmed POST applies), User management and self-protection, the dashboard counts and filters.\n- **Seam 2, Export:** `render_site(tmp_path, export_date=...)`, asserting on the output files. This covers Drafts never being written, the Current menu versus the Menu archive, the Upcoming boundary (the weekend tournament on its last day, and the day after), Navigation order, Spanish dates in Lima time, no Author names, sanitized Markdown (a `<script>` is stripped and an external image survives), and relative paths only.\n- **Fixture change:** each test gets a fresh temporary database seeded with the `DEMO_USERS` admin and editor from the conftest file, so `client_as` works and tests never share state.\n- **Prior art:** `tests/test_t00.py`, which checks the admin page answers over HTTP, that `render_site` writes files, and that paths are relative. `tests/conftest.py` provides the `client_as` pattern. The T00 tests must keep passing.\n- No tests below these two seams.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Media uploads. Images are only Markdown links to files hosted elsewhere.\n- Review or approval workflows, and any status beyond Draft and Published.\n- Scheduled publishing.\n- The office running Exports; there's no Export button in the console.\n- JavaScript on the public site, including client-side Upcoming filtering.\n- Member logins, comments and search.\n- Self-service password change and password reset.\n- Deleting Users.\n- Changing a Post's kind after creation.\n- Multi-site, drag-and-drop page builders and WYSIWYG editors (excluded by the assignment).\n- A Spanish admin console.\n- Deploying the admin console anywhere public (ADR-001).\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- The CLI keeps the assignment's command names. In the glossary, running `cms publish` and then `cms deploy` is one Export. The word \"publish\" in the console only ever means the Draft → Published status change.\n- A stale Upcoming list between Exports is accepted: the fix is to Export often, not to add JavaScript.\n- Confirmation is the club's main defence against a wrong price. It applies to Published content edits as well as status changes, because a Live Menu is most often corrected by editing it.\n- Every console route must require login. The permission check applies to routes, not to navigation links.\n\n.agents/skills/ask-matt/PHASE-BOUNDARIES.md\n.agents/skills/ask-matt/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/ask-matt/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/claude-handoff/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/claude-handoff/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/code-review/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/code-review/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/codebase-design/DEEPENING.md\n.agents/skills/codebase-design/DESIGN-IT-TWICE.md\n.agents/skills/codebase-design/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/codebase-design/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/diagnosing-bugs/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/diagnosing-bugs/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/diagnosing-bugs/scripts/hitl-loop.template.sh\n.agents/skills/domain-modeling/ADR-FORMAT.md\n.agents/skills/domain-modeling/CONTEXT-FORMAT.md\n.agents/skills/domain-modeling/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/domain-modeling/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/git-guardrails-claude-code/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/git-guardrails-claude-code/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/git-guardrails-claude-code/scripts/block-dangerous-git.sh\n.agents/skills/grill-me/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/grill-me/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/grill-with-docs/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/grill-with-docs/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/grilling/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/grilling/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/handoff/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/handoff/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/implement-spec/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/implement-spec/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/implement/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/implement/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/improve-codebase-architecture/HTML-REPORT.md\n.agents/skills/improve-codebase-architecture/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/improve-codebase-architecture/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/loop-me/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/loop-me/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/migrate-to-shoehorn/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/migrate-to-shoehorn/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/pr/CREDITS.md\n.agents/skills/pr/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/pr/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/prototype/LOG As a Member, I want a Slug to never change once content has been Published, even after it's Unpublished and republished, so that links shared on WhatsApp keep working.\n18. As an Editor, I want a clash with an existing Slug to be fixed automatically by adding a number (`-2`, `-3`), so that I can reuse a common title such as \"Menú de la semana\" every week.\n19. As an Editor, I want a clear error if I type a Slug that's already taken, so that I don't overwrite someone else's address.\n20. As an Editor, I want to preview a Post as Members will see it (Spanish public layout, sanitized Markdown), so that I catch mistakes before they go Live.\n21. As an Editor, I want to edit, publish, unpublish or delete any Post, including one written by another Editor, so that whoever is in the office can fix Friday's menu on Monday.\n22. As the club, I want the Author to stay the original writer when someone else edits a Post, so that the console records who created it.\n23. As an Admin, I want to do anything to Posts that an Editor can, so that I can cover for the office.\n\n### Events\n\n24. As an Editor, I want to give an Event a start date, an optional start time and an optional end date, so that a dinner at 8 pm and a Saturday–Sunday tournament are both described precisely.\n25. As an Editor, I want an Event without a start date to be rejected, so that every Event can be sorted.\n26. As an Editor, I want an end date earlier than the start date to be rejected with a clear message, so that impossible ranges never go Live.\n27. As a Member, I want the Event date shown first and prominently on every Event, so that I can tell at a glance when it is.\n28. As a Member, I want Upcoming Events listed soonest first, so that \"when is the next event?\" is answered at the top.\n29. As a Member, I want a weekend tournament to stay Upcoming until its end date has passed, so that I can still find it while it's happening.\n\n### Menu\n\n30. As an Editor, I want to post each week's menu as a new Menu Post, so that updating it takes a few minutes.\n31. As a Member, I want the Home page to show only the Current menu, so that I never read last week's prices by mistake.\n32. As a Member, I want older Menus in a Menu archive, so that I can look up what was served before.\n33. As an Editor, I want the previous Menu to leave the Home page automatically when the new one goes Live, so that nobody has to remember to unpublish it.\n\n### Confirmation\n\n34. As an Editor, I want publishing to go through a Confirmation screen showing the preview, so that an unfinished post or a wrong price isn't published by one careless click.\n35. As an Editor, I want saving edits to Published content to go through the same Confirmation, so that a price change to a Live menu is checked before it goes out.\n36. As an Editor, I want unpublishing to need Confirmation, so that I don't accidentally take down something Members rely on.\n37. As an Editor, I want deleting to need Confirmation that says it's permanent, so that I don't lose content by mistake.\n38. As an Editor, I want to cancel on the Confirmation screen and return to my unsaved edits, so that I can fix the mistake I just noticed.\n39. As an Editor, I want saving a Draft to need no Confirmation, so that everyday writing stays quick.\n\n### Pages\n\n40. As an Admin, I want to create, edit, preview, publish, unpublish and delete Pages (rules, membership, about) with the same Draft/Published lifecycle and Confirmation as Posts, so that I only learn one workflow.\n41. As an Admin, I want to give each Page a navigation order number, so that Members see the Pages in a sensible order.\n42. As a Member, I want every Live Page to appear in the Navigation, so that I can find the rules and membership information.\n43. As the club, I want a Page Slug that clashes with a public-site address (`index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`) to be rejected, so that a Page can never overwrite the Home page or a feed.\n\n### Users\n\n44. As an Admin, I want to create a User with a name, email, role and initial password, so that new office staff can start posting.\n45. As an Admin, I want to change a User's role between Admin and Editor, so that responsibilities can change.\n46. As an Admin, I want to deactivate and reactivate a User, so that former staff lose access without their content or authorship being lost.\n47. As an Admin, I want to be prevented from deactivating or demoting myself, so that the club always has an Admin.\n48. As an Admin, I want to see all Users with their role and whether they're active, so that I know who has access.\n49. As the club, I want Users never to be deleted, so that every Post and Page keeps its Author.\n\n### Dashboard and content lists\n\n50. As a User, I want a dashboard with counts of Posts by kind and status, and (for Admins) Pages by status, so that I can see the state of the site at a glance.\n51. As a User, I want the dashboard to show how many changes are not yet Live and when the last Export happened, so that I know when to ask for an Export.\n52. As a User, I want to filter the Post list by kind and by status, so that I can find this week's Draft menu quickly.\n53. As a User, I want the Post list to show title, kind, status, Author and last-updated time in Lima time, so that I can tell what changed recently.\n54. As a User, I want status filters that match no content to still appear, showing zero, so that the list doesn't change shape the way WordPress's did.\n\n### Public site\n\n55. As a Member, I want a Home page with the Current menu, the next Upcoming Events and the latest News, so that I can find what I need in a few seconds.\n56. As a Member, I want News, Events and Menu archive feeds in the Navigation, so that I can browse each kind of Post.\n57. As a Member, I want the Events feed to show Upcoming Events first and then past Events, most recent first, so that the list stays useful after an event is over.\n58. As a Member, I want the public site entirely in Spanish, dates included (\"29 de septiembre de 2026\"), so that it reads naturally.\n59. As a Member, I want every date and time shown in Lima time, so that a post never appears dated tomorrow.\n60. As a Member, I want News and Menus dated by when they were first published, so that the date means \"when the club said this\".\n61. As the club, I want no Author names on the public site, so that the club speaks as one voice.\n62. As the club, I want Drafts never to appear on the public site in any form (page, feed entry, Home page or Navigation), so that the club's third fear can't happen.\n63. As the club, I want user-written Markdown sanitized before it's rendered, so that a pasted `<script>` can't run in a Member's browser.\n64. As an Editor, I want to embed an image hosted elsewhere (for example on the club's Instagram) through a Markdown image link, so that posts can have pictures without uploads.\n65. As a Member, I want the site to work from its GitHub Pages subfolder, so that every link and stylesheet loads.\n\n### Export\n\n66. As the developer, I want an Export to render only Published content into `site/` and deploy it, so that what Members see is exactly what is Published.\n67. As the developer, I want each Export to decide Upcoming from the export date in Lima time, so that the result is predictable.\n68. As the developer, I want each Export to record when it ran, so that the console can count changes that aren't Live yet.\n69. As a grader, I want the seed script to create an Admin, an Editor and demo content (at least one Draft and one Published item of each kind, including a multi-day Event), so that the CMS can be tried right away from a fresh clone.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n### Modules\n\n- **Accounts:** Users, argon2 hashing, login and logout, the signed session cookie, and the rule that a Deactivated User is refused both at login and on every request.\n- **Permissions:** the role check shared by all console routes. Editor: Posts and the dashboard. Admin: everything. It's enforced server-side, and a refusal returns 403.\n- **CSRF:** a per-session token, a hidden field in every state-changing form, and a check on every non-GET console request.\n- **Content:** Posts and Pages, their Draft/Published lifecycle, Slug generation, clash handling and locking, Event-date validation, and the queries for Current menu, Upcoming and the feeds. The console and the Export share it, so both use the same definitions.\n- **Markdown rendering:** one function, used by both Preview and Export, that turns Markdown into sanitized HTML (markdown-it, then nh3). It allows `img` only with an `http(s)` source.\n- **Console routes and templates:** login, dashboard, Post list and editor, Page list and editor, Users, and Confirmation screens. The console is in English.\n- **Export:** `render_site` reads the database and writes the Spanish public site. The `cms publish` and `cms deploy` commands together make up an Export.\n- **Time:** helpers for UTC storage, America/Lima display (standard-library `zoneinfo`) and Spanish date formatting from a hard-coded month list. No new dependency.\n\n### Schema (SQLite; no ORM, following ADR-002's stack)\n\n- **users:** id, name, email (unique, case-insensitive), password_hash, role (`admin` | `editor`), active, created_at.\n- **posts:** id, kind (`news` | `event` | `menu`, fixed at creation), title, slug, body_md, status (`draft` | `published`), author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at, and for Events only: event_start_date, event_start_time (nullable), event_end_date (nullable). The slug is unique within a kind.\n- **pages:** id, title, slug (unique), body_md, status, nav_order, author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at.\n- **exports:** the time each Export ran, plus a count of Published items deleted since then, used by the not-yet-Live indicator.\n- All timestamps are stored in UTC.\n\n### Rules\n\n- **Slug:** generated from the title, lower-case ASCII with accents stripped and hyphens between words. An auto-generated clash gets a numeric suffix; a slug typed by hand that clashes is a form error. The slug can be edited only while `first_published_at` is empty, and once set, that field is never cleared, even by Unpublishing.\n- **Reserved Page Slugs:** `index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`.\n- **Event date:** a start date is required. The end date must be on or after the start date. Event fields are rejected on News and Menu Posts.\n- **Upcoming:** `coalesce(event_end_date, event_start_date) >= export_date`, where the export date is a date in America/Lima. The Home page shows the next 3 Upcoming Events.\n- **Current menu:** the Published Menu with the latest `first_published_at`. Every other Published Menu goes to the Menu archive, newest first.\n- **Home page:** the Current menu, the next 3 Upcoming Events and the latest 5 News, all on one page.\n- **Confirmation (no JavaScript):** submitting a change that Members will see (publish, unpublish, delete, or saving Published content) returns a Confirmation page. That page shows a summary and, where relevant, the rendered preview, and carries the pending values in hidden fields with the CSRF token. Only a second POST that includes the confirm flag applies the change. Cancel returns to the editor with the values intact. Saving a Draft applies immediately.\n- **Self-protection:** a request that would deactivate the Admin making it, or change that Admin's own role, is refused with an explanation.\n- **Not-yet-Live indicator:** Posts and Pages whose `updated_at` is after the last Export, plus Published items deleted since then. If there has never been an Export, every Published item counts.\n- **First Admin:** the seed script creates the first Admin and Editor, taking their passwords from `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` and `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD`. No separate bootstrap command.\n\n### Public site layout (all links relative)\n\n- `index.html`: the Home page.\n- `noticias/index.html` and `noticias/<slug>.html`: News.\n- `eventos/index.html` and `eventos/<slug>.html`: Events (Upcoming, then past).\n- `menus/index.html` and `menus/<slug>.html`: the Menu archive and each Menu.\n- `<slug>.html`: Pages.\n- `style.css`.\n- Navigation order: Inicio, Noticias, Eventos, Menús anteriores, then Pages by nav_order (ties broken by title).\n- The site title comes from `CMS_SITE_TITLE`, set to the club's name in `.env.example`.\n\n### Interfaces that change\n\n- `create_app()` accepts a database path (default from settings), so tests can give each app its own database.\n- `render_site(out, export_date=None)` reads Published content from the configured database. `export_date` defaults to today in America/Lima and records the Export.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **A good test** exercises behaviour through a public seam and asserts only what a User or Member could observe: status codes, redirects, rendered console HTML, and the files and HTML written to `site/`. Tests never query tables or call internal helpers. That lets the storage and module layout change without rewriting tests.\n- **Seam 1, the console over HTTP:** `create_app()` driven by the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures. This covers login and deactivation, Permissions (Editor gets 403 on Pages and Users), CSRF rejection, the Post and Page lifecycle, Slug rules, Event-date validation, Confirmation (the first POST changes nothing; the confirmed POST applies), User management and self-protection, the dashboard counts and filters.\n- **Seam 2, Export:** `render_site(tmp_path, export_date=...)`, asserting on the output files. This covers Drafts never being written, the Current menu versus the Menu archive, the Upcoming boundary (the weekend tournament on its last day, and the day after), Navigation order, Spanish dates in Lima time, no Author names, sanitized Markdown (a `<script>` is stripped and an external image survives), and relative paths only.\n- **Fixture change:** each test gets a fresh temporary database seeded with the `DEMO_USERS` admin and editor from the conftest file, so `client_as` works and tests never share state.\n- **Prior art:** `tests/test_t00.py`, which checks the admin page answers over HTTP, that `render_site` writes files, and that paths are relative. `tests/conftest.py` provides the `client_as` pattern. The T00 tests must keep passing.\n- No tests below these two seams.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Media uploads. Images are only Markdown links to files hosted elsewhere.\n- Review or approval workflows, and any status beyond Draft and Published.\n- Scheduled publishing.\n- The office running Exports; there's no Export button in the console.\n- JavaScript on the public site, including client-side Upcoming filtering.\n- Member logins, comments and search.\n- Self-service password change and password reset.\n- Deleting Users.\n- Changing a Post's kind after creation.\n- Multi-site, drag-and-drop page builders and WYSIWYG editors (excluded by the assignment).\n- A Spanish admin console.\n- Deploying the admin console anywhere public (ADR-001).\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- The CLI keeps the assignment's command names. In the glossary, running `cms publish` and then `cms deploy` is one Export. The word \"publish\" in the console only ever means the Draft → Published status change.\n- A stale Upcoming list between Exports is accepted: the fix is to Export often, not to add JavaScript.\n- Confirmation is the club's main defence against a wrong price. It applies to Published content edits as well as status changes, because a Live Menu is most often corrected by editing it.\n- Every console route must require login. The permission check applies to routes, not to navigation links.\n\n.agents/skills/ask-matt/PHASE-BOUNDARIES.md\n.agents/skills/ask-matt/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/ask-matt/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/claude-handoff/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/claude-handoff/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/code-review/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/code-review/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/codebase-design/DEEPENING.md\n.agents/skills/codebase-design/DESIGN-IT-TWICE.md\n.agents/skills/codebase-design/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/codebase-design/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/diagnosing-bugs/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/diagnosing-bugs/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/diagnosing-bugs/scripts/hitl-loop.template.sh\n.agents/skills/domain-modeling/ADR-FORMAT.md\n.agents/skills/domain-modeling/CONTEXT-FORMAT.md\n.agents/skills/domain-modeling/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/domain-modeling/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/git-guardrails-claude-code/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/git-guardrails-claude-code/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/git-guardrails-claude-code/scripts/block-dangerous-git.sh\n.agents/skills/grill-me/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/grill-me/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/grill-with-docs/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/grill-with-docs/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/grilling/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/grilling/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/handoff/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/handoff/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/implement-spec/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/implement-spec/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/implement/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/implement/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/improve-codebase-architecture/HTML-REPORT.md\n.agents/skills/improve-codebase-architecture/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/improve-codebase-architecture/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/loop-me/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/loop-me/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/migrate-to-shoehorn/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/migrate-to-shoehorn/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/pr/CREDITS.md\n.agents/skills/pr/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/pr/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/prototype/LOG Use when the user wants to build features or fix bugs test-first, mentions \"red-green-refactor\", or wants integration tests.\n---\n\n# Test-Driven Development\n\nTDD is the red → green loop. This skill is the reference that makes that loop produce tests worth keeping: what a good test is, where tests go, the anti-patterns, and the rules of the loop. Every section applies on every cycle: consult them before and during the loop, not after.\n\nWhen exploring the codebase, read `CONTEXT.md` (if it exists) so test names and interface vocabulary match the project's domain language, and respect ADRs in the area you're touching.\n\n## What a good test is\n\nTests verify behavior through public interfaces, not implementation details. Code can change entirely; tests shouldn't. A good test reads like a specification: \"user can checkout with valid cart\" tells you exactly what capability exists, and it survives refactors because it doesn't care about internal structure.\n\nSee [tests.md](tests.md) for examples and [mocking.md](mocking.md) for mocking guidelines.\n\n## Seams: where tests go\n\nA **seam** is the public boundary you test at: the interface where you observe behavior without reaching inside. Tests live at seams, never against internals.\n\n**Test only at pre-agreed seams.** Before writing any test, write down the seams under test and confirm them with the user. No test is written at an unconfirmed seam. You can't test everything, so agreeing the seams up front is how testing effort lands on the critical paths and complex logic instead of every edge case.\n\nAsk: \"What's the public interface, and which seams should we test?\"\n\nWhen the shape of that interface is itself in question (how deep the module is, where the seam belongs, what the interface should expose), call the Skill tool with \"codebase-design\" for the vocabulary. It is the shared source of the module, interface, depth, seam, adapter, leverage and locality terms, and it is a reference to consult, not a session to run.\n\n## Anti-patterns\n\n- **Implementation-coupled**: mocks internal collaborators, tests private methods, or verifies through a side channel (querying the database instead of using the interface). The tell: the test breaks when you refactor but behavior hasn't changed.\n- **Tautological**: the assertion recomputes the expected value the way the code does (`expect(add(a, b)).toBe(a + b)`, a snapshot derived by hand the same way, a constant asserted equal to itself), so it passes by construction and can never disagree with the code. Expected values must come from an independent source of truth: a known-good literal, a worked example, the spec.\n- **Horizontal slicing**: writing all tests first, then all implementation. Bulk tests verify _imagined_ behavior: you test the _shape_ of things rather than user-facing behavior, the tests go insensitive to real changes, and you commit to test structure before understanding the implementation. Work in **vertical slices** instead: one test → one implementation → repeat, each test a **tracer bullet** that responds to what the last cycle taught you.\n\n## Rules of the loop\n\n- **Red before green.** Write the failing test first, then only enough code to pass it. Don't anticipate future tests or add speculative features.\n- **One slice at a time.** One seam, one test, one minimal implementation per cycle.\n- **Refactoring is not part of the loop.** It belongs to the review stage (see the `code-review` skill), not the red → green implementation cycle.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"9a296da4-1451-4c83-a52f-f4ae68d4cb41","timestamp":"2026-10-04T16:01:09.171Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"app/__init__.py\napp/accounts.py\napp/auth.py\napp/cli.py\napp/content.py\napp/db.py\napp/main.py\napp/markdown.py\napp/publish.py\napp/routes/__init__.py\napp/routes/auth.py\napp/routes/console.py\napp/routes/posts.py\napp/settings.py\napp/timeutil.py\napp/web.py\ntests/conftest.py\ntests/test_ctx_guard.py\ntests/test_settings.py\ntests/test_t00.py\ntests/test_t01_login.py\ntests/test_t02_posts.py\ntests/test_t03_publish_export.py\ntests/test_t04_events_menu.py\ntests/test_usage_report.py\n    6 app/__init__.py\n   86 app/accounts.py\n   76 app/auth.py\n   56 app/cli.py\n  188 app/content.py\n   66 app/db.py\n   50 app/main.py\n   13 app/markdown.py\n  132 app/publish.py\n   34 app/settings.py\n   55 app/timeutil.py\n   25 app/web.py\n    0 app/routes/__init__.py\n   40 app/routes/auth.py\n   44 app/routes/console.py\n  121 app/routes/posts.py\n   80 tests/conftest.py\n   98 tests/test_ctx_guard.py\n   31 tests/test_settings.py\n   32 tests/test_t00.py\n  183 tests/test_t01_login.py\n  183 tests/test_t02_posts.py\n  169 tests/test_t03_publish_export.py\n  266 tests/test_t04_events_menu.py\n   69 tests/test_usage_report.py\n 2103 total\n---\nname: tdd\ndescription: Test-driven development. Editors and Admins can do all of it.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth, content\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.timeutil import fecha_evento\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/posts\")\n\nKIND_LABELS = {\"news\": \"News\", \"event\": \"Event\", \"menu\": \"Menu\"}\nKIND_LABELS_ES = {\"news\": \"Noticia\", \"event\": \"Evento\", \"menu\": \"Menú de la semana\"}\n\n\ndef form_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, post: dict, *,\n              error: str | None = None, status_code: int = 200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_form.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit post\" if post.get(\"id\") else \"New post\", \"user\": user,\n         \"post\": post, \"error\": error,\n         \"kind_labels\": KIND_LABELS},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\ndef load(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    post = content.get_post(conn, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"Post not found\")\n    return post\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef post_list(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_list.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Posts\", \"user\": user, \"posts\": content.list_posts(conn),\n         \"kind_labels\": KIND_LABELS})\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_post(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return form_page(request, user, {\"kind\": \"news\", \"event_end_date\": \"\"})\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef create_post(request: Request, kind: str = Form(\"\"), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n                slug: str = Form(\"\"), body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n                event_start_date: str = Form(\"\"), event_start_time: str = Form(\"\"),\n                event_end_date: str = Form(\"\"),\n                user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    event = dict(event_start_date=event_start_date, event_start_time=event_start_time,\n                 event_end_date=event_end_date)\n    try:\n        post_id = content.create_post(conn, kind=kind, title=title, slug=slug,\n                                      body_md=body_md, author_id=user[\"id\"], **event)\n    except content.PostError as exc:\n        return form_page(request, user, {\"kind\": kind, \"title\": title,\n                                         \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body_md, **event},\n                         error=str(exc), status_code=422)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef edit_post(request: Request, post_id: int,\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return form_page(request, user, dict(load(conn, post_id)))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef save_post(request: Request, post_id: int, title: str = Form(\"\"),\n              slug: str = Form(\"\"), body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n              event_start_date: str = Form(\"\"), event_start_time: str = Form(\"\"),\n              event_end_date: str = Form(\"\"),\n              user: sqlite3.Row = content.PostError as exc:\n        return form_page(request, user, {**dict(post), \"title\": title,\n                                         \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body_md, **event},\n                         error=str(exc), status_code=422)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/publish\")\ndef publish_post(post_id: int, \"lang\": \"es\", \"css_path\": \"/style.css\",\n         \"home_path\": \"/\", \"post\": post, \"date_label\": None,\n         \"event_label\": fecha_evento(post[\"event_start_date\"], post[\"event_start_time\"],\n                                     post[\"event_end_date\"]),\n         \"kind_label\": KIND_LABELS_ES[post[\"kind\"]],\n         \"body_html\": render_markdown(post[\"body_md\"])})\n\"\"\"Posts: creation, editing, Slugs and the list. Shared by the console and Export.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nimport unicodedata\nfrom datetime import date, time\n\nfrom app.timeutil import utc_now\n\nKINDS = (\"news\", \"event\", \"menu\")\n\n\nclass PostError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A problem with what the User typed, worded for the form.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef slugify(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Lower-case ASCII, accents stripped, hyphens between words.\"\"\"\n    plain = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", text).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", plain.lower()).strip(\"-\")\n\n\ndef _taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str, slug: str,\n           except_id: int | None) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE kind = ? AND slug = ? AND id IS NOT ?\",\n                        (kind, slug, except_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef _slug_for(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str, title: str, typed: str,\n              except_id: int | None = None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A typed Slug must be free; a generated one gets -2, -3, ... until it is.\"\"\"\n    if typed.strip():\n        slug = slugify(typed)\n        if not slug:\n            raise PostError(\"The slug needs letters or numbers.\")\n        if _taken(conn, kind, slug, except_id):\n            raise PostError(f\"The slug “{slug}” is already used by another post of this kind.\")\n        return slug\n    base = slugify(title)\n    if not base:\n        raise PostError(\"The title needs letters or numbers to make a slug from.\")\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while _taken(conn, kind, slug, except_id):\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef _clean_title(title: str) -> str:\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise PostError(\"The title is required.\")\n    return title\n\n\ndef _parsed(value: str, parse, label: str):\n    try:\n        return parse(value)\n    except ValueError:\n        raise PostError(f\"The {label} isn’t valid.\") from None\n\n\ndef _clean_event(kind: str, start_date: str, start_time: str,\n                 end_date: str) -> tuple[str | None, str | None, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"The Event date fields as stored. Only Events have them, and Events need a start date.\"\"\"\n    typed = [v.strip() for v in (start_date, start_time, end_date)]\n    if kind != \"event\":\n        if any(typed):\n            raise PostError(\"Event dates only apply to Events.\")\n        return None, None, None\n    start_date, start_time, end_date = typed\n    if not start_date:\n        raise PostError(\"An Event needs a start date.\")\n    start = _parsed(start_date, date.fromisoformat, \"start date\")\n    at = _parsed(start_time, time.fromisoformat, \"start time\") if start_time else None\n    end = _parsed(end_date, date.fromisoformat, \"end date\") if end_date else None\n    if end and end < start:\n        raise PostError(\"The end date can’t be earlier than the start date.\")\n    return (start.isoformat(), at.strftime(\"%H:%M\") if at else None,\n            end.isoformat() if end else None)\n\n\ndef create_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, kind: str, title: str, slug: str,\n                body_md: str, author_id: int, event_start_date: str = \"\",\n                event_start_time: str = \"\", event_end_date: str = \"\") -> int:\n    if kind not in KINDS:\n        raise PostError(\"Choose News, Event or Menu.\")\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    event = _clean_event(kind, event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date)\n    slug = _slug_for(conn, kind, title, slug)\n    now = utc_now()\n    cur = conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO posts (kind, title, slug, body_md, author_id, created_at, \"\n        \"updated_at, event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date) \"\n        \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (kind, title, slug, body_md, author_id, now, now, *event))\n    conn.commit()\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str,\n                slug: str, body_md: str, event_start_date: str = \"\",\n                event_start_time: str = \"\", event_end_date: str = \"\") -> None:\n    \"\"\"Kind and Author never change. The Slug is locked once first published.\"\"\"\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    event = _clean_event(post[\"kind\"], event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date)\n    if post[\"first_published_at\"]:\n        slug = post[\"slug\"]\n    else:\n        slug = _slug_for(conn, post[\"kind\"], title, slug or post[\"slug\"], post[\"id\"])\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, slug = ?, body_md = ?, updated_at = ?, \"\n                 \"event_start_date = ?, event_start_time = ?, event_end_date = ? \"\n                 \"WHERE id = ?\", (title, slug, body_md, utc_now(), *event, post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef get_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT posts.*, users.name AS author_name FROM posts \"\n        \"JOIN users ON users.id = posts.author_id \"\n        \"ORDER BY posts.updated_at DESC, posts.id DESC\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef publish_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Mark as Published. The first-published time is set once and never cleared.\"\"\"\n    now = utc_now()\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = 'published', updated_at = ?, \"\n                 \"first_published_at = coalesce(first_published_at, ?) WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (now, now, post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef unpublish_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Back to Draft. first_published_at stays, so the Slug stays locked.\"\"\"\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = 'draft', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (utc_now(), post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef published_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str | None = None,\n                    limit: int | None = None) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"Published Posts, newest first. Selects no Author: the public site has none.\"\"\"\n    sql = (\"SELECT id, kind, title, slug, body_md, first_published_at, \"\n           \"event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date FROM posts \"\n           \"WHERE status = 'published'\")\n    args: list = []\n    if kind:\n        sql += \" AND kind = ?\"\n        args.append(kind)\n    sql += \" ORDER BY first_published_at DESC, id DESC\"\n    if limit:\n        sql += \" LIMIT ?\"\n        args.append(limit)\n    return conn.execute(sql, args).fetchall()\n\n\ndef published_events(conn: sqlite3.Connection,\n                     export_date: date) -> tuple[list[sqlite3.Row], list[sqlite3.Row]]:\n    \"\"\"(Upcoming, past) Published Events.\n\n    Upcoming: the end date, or the start date if there is none, is on or after\n    the export date. Upcoming is soonest first; past is most recent first.\n    \"\"\"\n    today = export_date.isoformat()\n\n    def last_day(event) -> str:\n        return event[\"event_end_date\"] or event[\"event_start_date\"] or \"\"\n\n    def starts(event) -> tuple:\n        return (event[\"event_start_date\"] or \"\", event[\"event_start_time\"] or \"\",\n                event[\"id\"])\n\n    events = published_posts(conn, \"event\")\n    upcoming = sorted((e for e in events if last_day(e) >= today), key=starts)\n    past = sorted((e for e in events if last_day(e) < today),\n                  key=lambda e: (last_day(e), *starts(e)), reverse=True)\n    return upcoming, past\n\n\ndef published_menus(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> tuple[sqlite3.Row | None, list[sqlite3.Row]]:\n    \"\"\"(Current menu, Menu archive): the newest Published Menu, then all the others.\"\"\"\n    menus = published_posts(conn, \"menu\")\n    return (menus[0] if menus else None), menus[1:]\n\"\"\"SQLite access. No ORM (ADR-002): a connection per request, plain SQL.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    name            TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    email           TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    password_hash   TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    role            TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    session_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    created_at      TEXT    NOT NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id                INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    kind              TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('news', 'event', 'menu')),\n    title             TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    slug              TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    body_md           TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status            TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft'\n                              CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id         INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    created_at        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    updated_at        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    first_published_at TEXT,\n    event_start_date  TEXT,\n    event_start_time  TEXT,\n    event_end_date    TEXT,\n    UNIQUE (kind, slug)\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS exports (\n    id     INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    ran_at TEXT NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path | str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    # Sync FastAPI dependencies and endpoints may run on different threads.\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n    return conn\n\n\nEVENT_COLUMNS = (\"event_start_date\", \"event_start_time\", \"event_end_date\")\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path | str) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n        # A database made before T04 has no Event date columns.\n        have = {row[\"name\"] for row in conn.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(posts)\")}\n        for column in EVENT_COLUMNS:\n            if column not in have:\n                conn.execute(f\"ALTER TABLE posts ADD COLUMN {column} TEXT\")\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Shared Jinja environment for the console.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport jinja2\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.auth import csrf_token\nfrom app.timeutil import lima\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\ntemplates.env.globals[\"csrf_token\"] = csrf_token\ntemplates.env.filters[\"lima\"] = lima\n\n\n@jinja2.pass_context\ndef path_for(context, name: str, **params) -> str:\n    \"\"\"The path of a named console route, e.g. path_for('edit_post', post_id=3).\n\n    Unlike Starlette's url_for it stays a path (\"/admin/posts/3\"), not a full URL.\n    \"\"\"\n    return context[\"request\"].app.url_path_for(name, **params)\n\n\ntemplates.env.globals[\"path_for\"] = path_for\n\"\"\"Permissions and CSRF: the checks shared by every route.\n\n- `csrf_protect` runs on every state-changing request in the app.\n- `current_user` is what makes a console route require login, and refuses a\n  Deactivated User on every request.\n- `require_admin` is the role check for Admin-only areas. Refusal is a 403.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hmac\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import accounts, db\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a request needs a signed-in User and has none.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.db_path)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    \"\"\"This session's CSRF token, created on first use.\"\"\"\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    sent = request.headers.get(\"x-csrf-token\")\n    if sent is None:\n        sent = (await request.form()).get(\"csrf_token\")\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if (not expected or not isinstance(sent, str)\n            or not hmac.compare_digest(sent, expected)):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n\n\ndef sign_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    # Fresh session and token on login, so nothing set before it carries over.\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n    request.session[\"session_version\"] = user[\"session_version\"]\n    request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request,\n                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = accounts.get_user(conn, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if (user is None or not user[\"active\"]\n            or user[\"session_version\"] != request.session.get(\"session_version\")):\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired()\n    return user\n\n\ndef require_admin(user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(current_user)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"This area is for Admins only.\")\n    return user\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe console lives under /admin (plus /login and /logout); the public site\nanswers at /. Add routes in their own modules under app/routes/ and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse, Response\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import auth, db, publish, settings\nfrom app.routes import auth as auth_routes\nfrom app.routes import console\nfrom app.web import templates\n\n\ndef create_app(database_path: Path | str | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    db_path = Path(database_path) if database_path else settings.DATABASE_PATH\n    db.init_db(db_path)\n\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\",\n                  dependencies=[Depends(auth.csrf_protect)])\n    app.state.db_path = db_path\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n                       max_age=8 * 60 * 60)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(auth.LoginRequired)\n    def to_login(request: Request, exc: auth.LoginRequired):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    @app.get(\"/style.css\")\n    def stylesheet():\n        # Lets the console's Preview load the public stylesheet.\n        return Response(publish.CSS, media_type=\"text/css\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"news\": []},\n        )\n\n    app.include_router(auth_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(console.router)\n    return app\n\"\"\"The console: every route here requires a signed-in User.\n\nThe router-level dependency is what guarantees that, so a new route can't\nforget it. Pages and Users are Admin-only placeholders until T06 and T07.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import auth\nfrom app.routes import posts\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.current_user)])\n# Before the catch-all 404 at the bottom of this file.\nrouter.include_router(posts.router)\n\n\ndef page(request: Request, template: str, user: sqlite3.Row, title: str):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, template,\n                                      {\"title\": title, \"user\": user})\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef dashboard(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/dashboard.html\", user, \"Dashboard\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\ndef pages(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/placeholder.html\", user, \"Pages\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/users\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\ndef users(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/placeholder.html\", user, \"Users\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{path:path}\")\ndef not_found(path: str):\n    # Signed-out visitors were already redirected; this is a real 404.\n    raise HTTPException(404, \"Not found\")\n(eval):1: no matches found: app/templates/*\ndocs/adr/ADR-001-local-admin-static-public.md\ntemplates/admin/dashboard.html\ntemplates/admin/layout.html\ntemplates/admin/login.html\ntemplates/admin/placeholder.html\ntemplates/admin/post_form.html\ntemplates/admin/post_list.html\ntemplates/base.html\ntemplates/public/events.html\ntemplates/public/feed.html\ntemplates/public/home.html\ntemplates/public/post.html","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"a46eae32-c751-4c31-a439-522b03d5ed12","timestamp":"2026-10-04T16:01:13.066Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"\"\"\"Post list, editor and Preview.
