Depends(auth.current_user),\n                conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    event = dict(event_start_date=event_start_date, event_start_time=event_start_time,\n                 event_end_date=event_end_date)\n    try:\n        post_id = content.create_post(conn, kind=kind, title=title, slug=slug,\n                                      body_md=body_md, author_id=user[\"id\"], **event)\n    except content.PostError as exc:\n        return form_page(request, user, {\"kind\": kind, \"title\": title,\n                                         \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body_md, **event},\n                         error=str(exc), status_code=422)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef edit_post(request: Request, post_id: int,\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return form_page(request, user, dict(load(conn, post_id)))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef save_post(request: Request, post_id: int, title: str = Form(\"\"),\n              slug: str = Form(\"\"), body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n              event_start_date: str = Form(\"\"), event_start_time: str = Form(\"\"),\n              event_end_date: str = Form(\"\"),\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    event = dict(event_start_date=event_start_date, event_start_time=event_start_time,\n                 event_end_date=event_end_date)\n    try:\n        content.update_post(conn, post, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md, **event)\n    except content.PostError as exc:\n        return form_page(request, user, {**dict(post), \"title\": title,\n                                         \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body_md, **event},\n                         error=str(exc), status_code=422)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/publish\")\ndef publish_post(post_id: int, conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    content.publish_post(conn, load(conn, post_id))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/unpublish\")\ndef unpublish_post(post_id: int, conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    content.unpublish_post(conn, load(conn, post_id))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/preview\")\ndef preview(request: Request, post_id: int,\n            conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"public/post.html\",\n        {\"title\": post[\"title\"], \"lang\": \"es\", \"css_path\": \"/style.css\",\n         \"home_path\": \"/\", \"post\": post, \"date_label\": None,\n         \"event_label\": fecha_evento(post[\"event_start_date\"], post[\"event_start_time\"],\n                                     post[\"event_end_date\"]),\n         \"kind_label\": KIND_LABELS_ES[post[\"kind\"]],\n         \"body_html\": render_markdown(post[\"body_md\"])})\n\"\"\"Posts: creation, editing, Slugs and the list. Shared by the console and Export.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nimport unicodedata\nfrom datetime import date, time\n\nfrom app.timeutil import utc_now\n\nKINDS = (\"news\", \"event\", \"menu\")\n\n\nclass PostError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A problem with what the User typed, worded for the form.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef slugify(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Lower-case ASCII, accents stripped, hyphens between words.\"\"\"\n    plain = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", text).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", plain.lower()).strip(\"-\")\n\n\ndef _taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str, slug: str,\n           except_id: int | None) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE kind = ? AND slug = ? AND id IS NOT ?\",\n                        (kind, slug, except_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef _slug_for(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str, title: str, typed: str,\n              except_id: int | None = None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A typed Slug must be free; a generated one gets -2, -3, ... until it is.\"\"\"\n    if typed.strip():\n        slug = slugify(typed)\n        if not slug:\n            raise PostError(\"The slug needs letters or numbers.\")\n        if _taken(conn, kind, slug, except_id):\n            raise PostError(f\"The slug “{slug}” is already used by another post of this kind.\")\n        return slug\n    base = slugify(title)\n    if not base:\n        raise PostError(\"The title needs letters or numbers to make a slug from.\")\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while _taken(conn, kind, slug, except_id):\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef _clean_title(title: str) -> str:\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise PostError(\"The title is required.\")\n    return title\n\n\ndef _parsed(value: str, parse, label: str):\n    try:\n        return parse(value)\n    except ValueError:\n        raise PostError(f\"The {label} isn’t valid.\") from None\n\n\ndef _clean_event(kind: str, start_date: str, start_time: str,\n                 end_date: str) -> tuple[str | None, str | None, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"The Event date fields as stored. Only Events have them, and Events need a start date.\"\"\"\n    typed = [v.strip() for v in (start_date, start_time, end_date)]\n    if kind != \"event\":\n        if any(typed):\n            raise PostError(\"Event dates only apply to Events.\")\n        return None, None, None\n    start_date, start_time, end_date = typed\n    if not start_date:\n        raise PostError(\"An Event needs a start date.\")\n    start = _parsed(start_date, date.fromisoformat, \"start date\")\n    at = _parsed(start_time, time.fromisoformat, \"start time\") if start_time else None\n    end = _parsed(end_date, date.fromisoformat, \"end date\") if end_date else None\n    if end and end < start:\n        raise PostError(\"The end date can’t be earlier than the start date.\")\n    return (start.isoformat(), at.strftime(\"%H:%M\") if at else None,\n            end.isoformat() if end else None)\n\n\ndef create_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, kind: str, title: str, slug: str,\n                body_md: str, author_id: int, event_start_date: str = \"\",\n                event_start_time: str = \"\", event_end_date: str = \"\") -> int:\n    if kind not in KINDS:\n        raise PostError(\"Choose News, Event or Menu.\")\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    event = _clean_event(kind, event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date)\n    slug = _slug_for(conn, kind, title, slug)\n    now = utc_now()\n    cur = conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO posts (kind, title, slug, body_md, author_id, created_at, \"\n        \"updated_at, event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date) \"\n        \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (kind, title, slug, body_md, author_id, now, now, *event))\n    conn.commit()\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str,\n                slug: str, body_md: str, event_start_date: str = \"\",\n                event_start_time: str = \"\", event_end_date: str = \"\") -> None:\n    \"\"\"Kind and Author never change. The Slug is locked once first published.\"\"\"\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    event = _clean_event(post[\"kind\"], event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date)\n    if post[\"first_published_at\"]:\n        slug = post[\"slug\"]\n    else:\n        slug = _slug_for(conn, post[\"kind\"], title, slug or post[\"slug\"], post[\"id\"])\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, slug = ?, body_md = ?, updated_at = ?, \"\n                 \"event_start_date = ?, event_start_time = ?, event_end_date = ? \"\n                 \"WHERE id = ?\", (title, slug, body_md, utc_now(), *event, post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef get_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT posts.*, users.name AS author_name FROM posts \"\n        \"JOIN users ON users.id = posts.author_id \"\n        \"ORDER BY posts.updated_at DESC, posts.id DESC\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef publish_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Mark as Published. The first-published time is set once and never cleared.\"\"\"\n    now = utc_now()\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = 'published', updated_at = ?, \"\n                 \"first_published_at = coalesce(first_published_at, ?) WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (now, now, post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef unpublish_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Back to Draft. first_published_at stays, so the Slug stays locked.\"\"\"\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = 'draft', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (utc_now(), post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef published_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str | None = None,\n                    limit: int | None = None) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"Published Posts, newest first. Selects no Author: the public site has none.\"\"\"\n    sql = (\"SELECT id, kind, title, slug, body_md, first_published_at, \"\n           \"event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date FROM posts \"\n           \"WHERE status = 'published'\")\n    args: list = []\n    if kind:\n        sql += \" AND kind = ?\"\n        args.append(kind)\n    sql += \" ORDER BY first_published_at DESC, id DESC\"\n    if limit:\n        sql += \" LIMIT ?\"\n        args.append(limit)\n    return conn.execute(sql, args).fetchall()\n\n\ndef published_events(conn: sqlite3.Connection,\n                     export_date: date) -> tuple[list[sqlite3.Row], list[sqlite3.Row]]:\n    \"\"\"(Upcoming, past) Published Events.\n\n    Upcoming: the end date, or the start date if there is none, is on or after\n    the export date. Upcoming is soonest first; past is most recent first.\n    \"\"\"\n    today = export_date.isoformat()\n\n    def last_day(event) -> str:\n        return event[\"event_end_date\"] or event[\"event_start_date\"] or \"\"\n\n    def starts(event) -> tuple:\n        return (event[\"event_start_date\"] or \"\", event[\"event_start_time\"] or \"\",\n                event[\"id\"])\n\n    events = published_posts(conn, \"event\")\n    upcoming = sorted((e for e in events if last_day(e) >= today), key=starts)\n    past = sorted((e for e in events if last_day(e) < today),\n                  key=lambda e: (last_day(e), *starts(e)), reverse=True)\n    return upcoming, past\n\n\ndef published_menus(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> tuple[sqlite3.Row | None, list[sqlite3.Row]]:\n    \"\"\"(Current menu, Menu archive): the newest Published Menu, then all the others.\"\"\"\n    menus = published_posts(conn, \"menu\")\n    return (menus[0] if menus else None), menus[1:]\n\"\"\"SQLite access. No ORM (ADR-002): a connection per request, plain SQL.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    name            TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    email           TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    password_hash   TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    role            TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    session_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    created_at      TEXT    NOT NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id                INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    kind              TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('news', 'event', 'menu')),\n    title             TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    slug              TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    body_md           TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status            TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft'\n                              CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id         INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    created_at        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    updated_at        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    first_published_at TEXT,\n    event_start_date  TEXT,\n    event_start_time  TEXT,\n    event_end_date    TEXT,\n    UNIQUE (kind, slug)\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS exports (\n    id     INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    ran_at TEXT NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path | str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    # Sync FastAPI dependencies and endpoints may run on different threads.\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n    return conn\n\n\nEVENT_COLUMNS = (\"event_start_date\", \"event_start_time\", \"event_end_date\")\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path | str) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n        # A database made before T04 has no Event date columns.\n        have = {row[\"name\"] for row in conn.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(posts)\")}\n        for column in EVENT_COLUMNS:\n            if column not in have:\n                conn.execute(f\"ALTER TABLE posts ADD COLUMN {column} TEXT\")\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Shared Jinja environment for the console.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport jinja2\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.auth import csrf_token\nfrom app.timeutil import lima\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\ntemplates.env.globals[\"csrf_token\"] = csrf_token\ntemplates.env.filters[\"lima\"] = lima\n\n\n@jinja2.pass_context\ndef path_for(context, name: str, **params) -> str:\n    \"\"\"The path of a named console route, e.g. path_for('edit_post', post_id=3).\n\n    Unlike Starlette's url_for it stays a path (\"/admin/posts/3\"), not a full URL.\n    \"\"\"\n    return context[\"request\"].app.url_path_for(name, **params)\n\n\ntemplates.env.globals[\"path_for\"] = path_for\n\"\"\"Permissions and CSRF: the checks shared by every route.\n\n- `csrf_protect` runs on every state-changing request in the app.\n- `current_user` is what makes a console route require login, and refuses a\n  Deactivated User on every request.\n- `require_admin` is the role check for Admin-only areas. Refusal is a 403.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hmac\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import accounts, db\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a request needs a signed-in User and has none.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.db_path)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    \"\"\"This session's CSRF token, created on first use.\"\"\"\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    sent = request.headers.get(\"x-csrf-token\")\n    if sent is None:\n        sent = (await request.form()).get(\"csrf_token\")\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if (not expected or not isinstance(sent, str)\n            or not hmac.compare_digest(sent, expected)):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n\n\ndef sign_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    # Fresh session and token on login, so nothing set before it carries over.\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n    request.session[\"session_version\"] = user[\"session_version\"]\n    request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request,\n                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = accounts.get_user(conn, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if (user is None or not user[\"active\"]\n            or user[\"session_version\"] != request.session.get(\"session_version\")):\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired()\n    return user\n\n\ndef require_admin(user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(current_user)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"This area is for Admins only.\")\n    return user\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe console lives under /admin (plus /login and /logout); the public site\nanswers at /. Add routes in their own modules under app/routes/ and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse, Response\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import auth, db, publish, settings\nfrom app.routes import auth as auth_routes\nfrom app.routes import console\nfrom app.web import templates\n\n\ndef create_app(database_path: Path | str | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    db_path = Path(database_path) if database_path else settings.DATABASE_PATH\n    db.init_db(db_path)\n\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\",\n                  dependencies=[Depends(auth.csrf_protect)])\n    app.state.db_path = db_path\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n                       max_age=8 * 60 * 60)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(auth.LoginRequired)\n    def to_login(request: Request, exc: auth.LoginRequired):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    @app.get(\"/style.css\")\n    def stylesheet():\n        # Lets the console's Preview load the public stylesheet.\n        return Response(publish.CSS, media_type=\"text/css\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"news\": []},\n        )\n\n    app.include_router(auth_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(console.router)\n    return app\n\"\"\"The console: every route here requires a signed-in User.\n\nThe router-level dependency is what guarantees that, so a new route can't\nforget it. Pages and Users are Admin-only placeholders until T06 and T07.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import auth\nfrom app.routes import posts\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.current_user)])\n# Before the catch-all 404 at the bottom of this file.\nrouter.include_router(posts.router)\n\n\ndef page(request: Request, template: str, user: sqlite3.Row, title: str):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, template,\n                                      {\"title\": title, \"user\": user})\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef dashboard(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/dashboard.html\", user, \"Dashboard\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\ndef pages(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/placeholder.html\", user, \"Pages\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/users\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\ndef users(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/placeholder.html\", user, \"Users\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{path:path}\")\ndef not_found(path: str):\n    # Signed-out visitors were already redirected; this is a real 404.\n    raise HTTPException(404, \"Not found\")\n(eval):1: no matches found: app/templates/*\ndocs/adr/ADR-001-local-admin-static-public.md\ntemplates/admin/dashboard.html\ntemplates/admin/layout.html\ntemplates/admin/login.html\ntemplates/admin/placeholder.html\ntemplates/admin/post_form.html\ntemplates/admin/post_list.html\ntemplates/base.html\ntemplates/public/events.html\ntemplates/public/feed.html\ntemplates/public/home.html\ntemplates/public/post.html","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"15ce88d6-cb03-4b9c-a1ec-900c3288bd5d","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":3},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"75d2ed53-52ff-49d6-8599-8d6753fa0013","session_id":"c9432616-ab73-4985-a1c5-6391db0fa022","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"e31a54a4-37c7-4d6f-a759-1926c25809ff","version":"2.1.285","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"be51f4e4-89ec-4404-99ad-c4a05a36eab2","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"96303632-9da0-49ca-b24e-0e1d26baa801","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QmM8Hi925vZ6uazdJpAxpG","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\nEach test gets its own temporary database, seeded with the demo Admin and\nEditor, so tests never share state.\n\n`client` gives you an anonymous client. `client_as(role)` gives you a client\nthat is signed in as a seeded user of that role, so access-control tests stay\none line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code == 403\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\n# A developer's real .env must not change test behaviour.\nos.environ.setdefault(\"CMS_ENV_FILE\", os.devnull)\n\nfrom app import settings  # noqa: E402\nfrom app.accounts import seed_users  # noqa: E402\nfrom app.main import create_app  # noqa: E402\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\nCSRF_FIELD = re.compile(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"')\n\n\ndef csrf_from(response) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Pull the CSRF token out of a rendered form.\"\"\"\n    match = CSRF_FIELD.search(response.text)\n    assert match, \"no csrf_token field in the page\"\n    return match.group(1)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef db_path(tmp_path):\n    path = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n    seed_users(path, admin_password=DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"],\n               editor_password=DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"password\"])\n    return path\n\n\n@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\ndef _configured_database(db_path, monkeypatch):\n    \"\"\"render_site reads the configured database; point it at this test's own.\"\"\"\n    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, \"DATABASE_PATH\", db_path)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(db_path) -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app(db_path))\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(db_path):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a signed-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app(db_path))\n        token = csrf_from(c.get(\"/login\"))\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"],\n                                          \"csrf_token\": token},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        assert response.status_code in (302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n\"\"\"T03: publish a Post, then Export it. Seam 1 (console) and Seam 2 (render_site).\"\"\"\nfrom datetime import date\n\nfrom app.publish import render_site\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_from\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import edit_post, save_post\n\nEXPORT_DAY = date(2026, 9, 29)\n\n\ndef new_post(client, **kw):\n    return save_post(client, **kw).headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef post_action(client, location, action):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(location))\n    return client.post(f\"{location}/{action}\", data={\"csrf_token\": token},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef publish(client, **kw):\n    location = new_post(client, **kw)\n    assert post_action(client, location, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    return location\n\n\ndef export(tmp_path):\n    return render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", export_date=EXPORT_DAY)\n\n\ndef all_html(out):\n    return \"\\n\".join(p.read_text() for p in out.rglob(\"*.html\"))\n\n\n# --- console -------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_editor_publishes_and_unpublishes(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    assert \"Draft\" in editor.get(location).text\n    assert post_action(editor, location, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Published\" in editor.get(location).text\n    assert post_action(editor, location, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n    page = editor.get(location).text\n    assert \"Draft\" in page and \"Published\" not in page\n\n\ndef test_publish_needs_a_csrf_token(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    assert editor.post(f\"{location}/publish\").status_code == 403\n    assert \"Draft\" in editor.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_slug_stays_locked_after_publish_and_unpublish(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\", slug=\"torneo\")\n    post_action(editor, location, \"unpublish\")\n    edit_post(editor, location, title=\"Torneo\", slug=\"otro\", body_md=\"x\")\n    assert \"readonly\" in editor.get(location).text\n    assert 'value=\"torneo\"' in editor.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_first_published_time_never_changes(client_as, monkeypatch):\n    from app import content\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    monkeypatch.setattr(content, \"utc_now\", lambda: \"2026-09-29T15:00:00+00:00\")\n    location = publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    assert \"First published (Lima): 2026-09-29 10:00\" in editor.get(location).text\n    monkeypatch.setattr(content, \"utc_now\", lambda: \"2026-10-05T15:00:00+00:00\")\n    post_action(editor, location, \"unpublish\")\n    post_action(editor, location, \"publish\")\n    assert \"First published (Lima): 2026-09-29 10:00\" in editor.get(location).text\n\n\n# --- Export --------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_drafts_never_reach_the_site(client_as, tmp_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    new_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Borrador secreto\", slug=\"borrador\")","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"0ec36ba5-ba96-45d6-aee6-c1e251fdeacd","timestamp":"2026-10-04T16:01:13.098Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\nEach test gets {"parentUuid":"e74c8bdd-fd39-46b4-be7c-ad64616e0749","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"96303632-9da0-49ca-b24e-0e1d26baa801","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QTns6JcigqwJPLUFr9ioix","type":"tool_result","content":"{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block console %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"{% if post.id %}/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}{% else %}/admin/posts{% endif %}\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n    {% if post.id %}\n      <p>Kind: {{ kind_labels[post.kind] }} · Status: {{ post.status | capitalize }}\n        {% if post.first_published_at %}· First published (Lima): {{ post.first_published_at | lima }}{% endif %}</p>\n    {% else %}\n      <p><label>Kind\n        <select name=\"kind\">\n          {% for value, label in kind_labels.items() %}\n            <option value=\"{{ value }}\"{% if post.kind == value %} selected{% endif %}>{{ label }}</option>\n          {% endfor %}\n        </select></label></p>\n    {% endif %}\n    <p><label>Title <input type=\"text\" name=\"title\" value=\"{{ post.title }}\" required></label></p>\n    <p><label>Slug <input type=\"text\" name=\"slug\" value=\"{{ post.slug }}\"\n      {% if post.first_published_at %}readonly{% endif %}></label>\n      {% if not post.id %}<small>Leave blank to generate it from the title.</small>{% endif %}</p>\n    {% if not post.id or post.kind == 'event' %}\n      <fieldset>\n        <legend>Event date{% if not post.id %} (Events only){% endif %}</legend>\n        <p><label>Start date <input type=\"date\" name=\"event_start_date\" value=\"{{ post.event_start_date or '' }}\"></label>\n          <label>Start time (optional) <input type=\"time\" name=\"event_start_time\" value=\"{{ post.event_start_time or '' }}\"></label>\n          <label>End date (optional) <input type=\"date\" name=\"event_end_date\" value=\"{{ post.event_end_date or '' }}\"></label></p>\n      </fieldset>\n    {% endif %}\n    <p><label>Body (Markdown)<br>\n      <textarea name=\"body_md\" rows=\"14\" cols=\"70\">{{ post.body_md }}</textarea></label></p>\n    <button type=\"submit\">Save draft</button>\n    {% if post.id %}<a href=\"{{ path_for('preview', post_id=post.id) }}\" target=\"_blank\">Preview</a>{% endif %}\n    <a href=\"{{ path_for('post_list') }}\">Back to posts</a>\n  </form>\n  {% if post.id %}\n    <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ path_for('unpublish_post' if post.status == 'published' else 'publish_post', post_id=post.id) }}\">\n      <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n      <button type=\"submit\">{% if post.status == 'published' %}Unpublish{% else %}Publish{% endif %}</button>\n    </form>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <nav>\n    <a href=\"{{ path_for('dashboard') }}\">Dashboard</a>\n    <a href=\"{{ path_for('post_list') }}\">Posts</a>\n    {% if user.role == \"admin\" %}\n      <a href=\"{{ path_for('pages') }}\">Pages</a>\n      <a href=\"{{ path_for('users') }}\">Users</a>\n    {% endif %}\n    <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ path_for('logout') }}\" style=\"display:inline\">\n      <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n      <button type=\"submit\">Sign out</button>\n    </form>\n  </nav>\n  {% block console %}{% endblock %}\n{% endblock %}\n\"\"\"T02: create and edit a Draft Post, through the console over HTTP (Seam 1).\"\"\"\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom zoneinfo import ZoneInfo\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_from\n\n\ndef save_post(client, *, title=\"Menú de la semana\", kind=\"menu\", body=\"Hola\",\n              slug=\"\"):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(\"/admin/posts/new\"))\n    # Since T04 an Event needs a start date; News and Menus must not have one.\n    event = {\"event_start_date\": \"2026-10-10\"} if kind == \"event\" else {}\n    return client.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\n        \"csrf_token\": token, \"kind\": kind, \"title\": title, \"slug\": slug,\n        \"body_md\": body, **event}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef edit_post(client, location, **fields):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(location))\n    return client.post(location, data={\"csrf_token\": token, **fields},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_editor_creates_a_draft_of_each_kind_with_themselves_as_author(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    for kind in (\"news\", \"event\", \"menu\"):\n        response = save_post(editor, title=f\"Un {kind}\", kind=kind)\n        assert response.status_code == 303\n    page = editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    for kind in (\"News\", \"Event\", \"Menu\"):\n        assert kind in page\n    assert page.count(\"Draft\") >= 3\n    assert page.count(\"Demo Editor\") >= 3\n\n\ndef test_post_forms_carry_the_csrf_token(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = save_post(editor).headers[\"location\"]\n    assert 'name=\"csrf_token\"' in editor.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").text\n    assert 'name=\"csrf_token\"' in editor.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_post_form_without_csrf_token_is_refused(client_as):\n    response = client_as(\"editor\").post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\n        \"kind\": \"news\", \"title\": \"x\", \"body_md\": \"y\"})\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_signed_out_visitor_is_sent_to_login(client):\n    assert client.get(\"/admin/posts\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_kind_cannot_be_changed_after_creation(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = save_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Solo noticia\").headers[\"location\"]\n    edit_post(editor, location, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Solo path.write_text(html)\n\n\ndef _event_label(post) -> str:\n    return fecha_evento(post[\"event_start_date\"], post[\"event_start_time\"],\n                        post[\"event_end_date\"])\n\n\ndef db.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    try:\n        posts = content.published_posts(conn)\n        upcoming, past = content.published_events(conn, export_date)\n        current_menu, archive = content.published_menus(conn)\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n\n    def page(template: str, depth: int, **ctx) -> str:\n        root = \"../\" * depth\n        nav = [(\"Inicio\", f\"{root}index.html\"),\n               (\"Noticias\", f\"{root}noticias/index.html\"),\n               (\"Eventos\", f\"{root}eventos/index.html\"),\n               (\"Menús anteriores\", f\"{root}menus/index.html\")]\n        return env.get_template(template).render(\n menu = None\n    if current_menu:\n        menu = {**_link(current_menu, \"\"),\n                \"body_html\": render_markdown(current_menu[\"body_md\"])}\n    _write(out, \"index.html\", page(\n home.index(\"Noticia 7\") < home.index(\"Noticia 6\") < home.index(\"Noticia 3\")\n\n\ndef test_news_index_lists_all_published_news(client_as, tmp_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    for n publish(editor, kind=\"event\", title=\"Un evento\")\n    publish(editor, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Un menú\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    assert (out / \"noticias\" / test_news_date_is_spanish_and_in_lima_time(client_as, tmp_path, monkeypatch):\n    from app import content\n    # 2026-09-30 02:30 UTC is still 29 September 21:30 in Lima.\n    monkeypatch.setattr(content, \"utc_now\", lambda: \"2026-09-30T02:30:00+00:00\")\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Noche de gala\")\n    out = \"editor@example.test\" not in html\n\n\ndef test_script_in_a_body_is_stripped_but_external_image_survives(client_as, tmp_path):\n    body = \"Hola <script>alert(1)</script>\\n\\n![foto](https://example.org/a.png)\"\n    publish(client_as(\"editor\"), kind=\"news\", title=\"Peligro\", body=body)\n    html = (export(tmp_path) / \"noticias\" / test_each_export_records_the_time_it_ran(client_as, tmp_path, db_path):\n    import sqlite3\n    export(tmp_path)\n    export(tmp_path)\n    rows = sqlite3.connect(db_path).execute(\"SELECT ran_at FROM exports\").fetchall()\n    assert len(rows) == 2 and all(r[0] for r in rows)\n\"\"\"Time: stored in UTC, shown in America/Lima.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom datetime import date, datetime, timezone\nfrom zoneinfo import ZoneInfo\n\nLIMA = ZoneInfo(\"America/Lima\")\n\n\ndef utc_now() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n\n\ndef lima(iso: str | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A stored UTC timestamp as 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM' in Lima time.\"\"\"\n    if not iso:\n        return \"\"\n    return datetime.fromisoformat(iso).astimezone(LIMA).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n\n\nMONTHS_ES = (\"enero\", \"febrero\", \"marzo\", \"abril\", \"mayo\", \"junio\", \"julio\",\n             \"agosto\", \"septiembre\", \"octubre\", \"noviembre\", \"diciembre\")\n\n\ndef today_lima() -> date:\n    return datetime.now(LIMA).date()\n\n\ndef fecha(iso: str | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A stored UTC timestamp as a Lima-time Spanish date: '29 de septiembre de 2026'.\"\"\"\n    if not iso:\n        return \"\"\n    d = datetime.fromisoformat(iso).astimezone(LIMA)\n    return f\"{d.day} de {MONTHS_ES[d.month - 1]} de {d.year}\"\n\n\ndef fecha_dia(day: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A stored Lima calendar date ('2026-10-03') as '3 de octubre de 2026'.\"\"\"\n    d = date.fromisoformat(day)\n    return f\"{d.day} de {MONTHS_ES[d.month - 1]} de {d.year}\"\n\n\ndef fecha_evento(start: str | None, start_time: str | None, end: str | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"An Event date: '3 de octubre de 2026, 20:00 al 4 de octubre de 2026'.\n\n    Event dates and times are already Lima local, so nothing is converted.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not start:\n        return \"\"\n    label = fecha_dia(start)\n    if start_time:\n        label += f\", {start_time}\"\n    if end and end != start:\n        label += f\" al {fecha_dia(end)}\"\n    return title=\"Noticia pública\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    assert \"Borrador secreto\" not in all_html(out)\n    assert not (out / \"noticias\" / \"borrador.html\").exists()\n    assert (out / \"noticias\" / \"noticia-publica.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_unpublished_post_leaves_the_site_at_next_export(client_as, tmp_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Retirada\")\n    post_action(editor, location, \"unpublish\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    assert \"Retirada\" not in all_html(out)\n\n\ndef test_home_shows_latest_five_news_newest_first(client_as, tmp_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    for n in range(1, 8):\n        publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=f\"Noticia {n}\")\n    home = (export(tmp_path) / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Noticia 7\" in home and \"Noticia 3\" in home\n    assert \"Noticia 2\" not in home and \"Noticia 1\" not in home\n    assert home.index(\"Noticia 7\") < home.index(\"Noticia 6\") < home.index(\"Noticia 3\")\n\n\ndef test_news_index_lists_all_published_news(client_as, tmp_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    for n in range(1, 8):\n        publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=f\"Noticia {n}\")\n    index = (export(tmp_path) / \"noticias\" / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert all(f\"Noticia {n}\" in index for n in range(1, 8))\n\n\ndef test_each_kind_gets_its_page_in_its_own_folder(client_as, tmp_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Una noticia\")\n    publish(editor, kind=\"event\", title=\"Un evento\")\n    publish(editor, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Un menú\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    assert (out / \"noticias\" / \"una-noticia.html\").exists()\n    assert (out / \"eventos\" / \"un-evento.html\").exists()\n    assert (out / \"menus\" / \"un-menu.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_news_date_is_spanish_and_in_lima_time(client_as, tmp_path, monkeypatch):\n    from app import content\n    # 2026-09-30 02:30 UTC is still 29 September 21:30 in Lima.\n    monkeypatch.setattr(content, \"utc_now\", lambda: \"2026-09-30T02:30:00+00:00\")\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Noche de gala\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    assert \"29 de septiembre de 2026\" in (out / \"noticias\" / \"noche-de-gala.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"29 de septiembre de 2026\" in (out / \"noticias\" / \"index.html\").read_text()\n\n\ndef test_no_author_name_anywhere_in_the_site(client_as, tmp_path):\n    publish(client_as(\"editor\"), kind=\"news\", title=\"Con autor\")\n    publish(client_as(\"admin\"), kind=\"news\", title=\"Con otro autor\")\n    html = all_html(export(tmp_path))\n    assert \"Demo Editor\" not in html and \"Demo Admin\" not in html\n    assert \"editor@example.test\" not in html\n\n\ndef test_script_in_a_body_is_stripped_but_external_image_survives(client_as, tmp_path):\n    body = \"Hola <script>alert(1)</script>\\n\\n![foto](https://example.org/a.png)\"\n    publish(client_as(\"editor\"), kind=\"news\", title=\"Peligro\", body=body)\n    html = (export(tmp_path) / \"noticias\" / \"peligro.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"<script\" not in html and \"alert(1)\" not in html\n    assert 'src=\"https://example.org/a.png\"' in html\n\n\ndef test_site_uses_relative_paths_only(client_as, tmp_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Ruta\", body=\"[a](/x) ![i](/y.png)\")\n    publish(editor, kind=\"event\", title=\"Otro\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    for path in out.rglob(\"*.html\"):\n        text = path.read_text()\n        assert 'href=\"/' not in text and 'src=\"/' not in text, path\n    assert 'href=\"../style.css\"' in (out / \"noticias\" / \"ruta.html\").read_text()\n    assert 'href=\"style.css\"' in (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n\n\ndef test_each_export_records_the_time_it_ran(client_as, tmp_path, db_path):\n    import sqlite3\n    export(tmp_path)\n    export(tmp_path)\n    rows = sqlite3.connect(db_path).execute(\"SELECT ran_at FROM exports\").fetchall()\n    assert len(rows) == 2 and all(r[0] for r in rows)\n\"\"\"Time: stored in UTC, shown in America/Lima.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom datetime import date, datetime, timezone\nfrom zoneinfo import ZoneInfo\n\nLIMA = ZoneInfo(\"America/Lima\")\n\n\ndef utc_now() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n\n\ndef lima(iso: str | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A stored UTC timestamp as 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM' in Lima time.\"\"\"\n    if not iso:\n        return \"\"\n    return datetime.fromisoformat(iso).astimezone(LIMA).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n\n\nMONTHS_ES = (\"enero\", \"febrero\", \"marzo\", \"abril\", \"mayo\", \"junio\", \"julio\",\n             \"agosto\", \"septiembre\", \"octubre\", \"noviembre\", \"diciembre\")\n\n\ndef today_lima() -> date:\n    return datetime.now(LIMA).date()\n\n\ndef fecha(iso: str | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A stored UTC timestamp as a Lima-time Spanish date: '29 de septiembre de 2026'.\"\"\"\n    if not iso:\n        return \"\"\n    d = datetime.fromisoformat(iso).astimezone(LIMA)\n    return f\"{d.day} de {MONTHS_ES[d.month - 1]} de {d.year}\"\n\n\ndef fecha_dia(day: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A stored Lima calendar date ('2026-10-03') as '3 de octubre de 2026'.\"\"\"\n    d = date.fromisoformat(day)\n    return f\"{d.day} de {MONTHS_ES[d.month - 1]} de {d.year}\"\n\n\ndef fecha_evento(start: str | None, start_time: str | None, end: str | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"An Event date: '3 de octubre de 2026, 20:00 al 4 de octubre de 2026'.\n\n    Event dates and times are already Lima local, so nothing is converted.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not start:\n        return \"\"\n    label = fecha_dia(start)\n    if start_time:\n        label += f\", {start_time}\"\n    if end and end != start:\n        label += f\" al {fecha_dia(end)}\"\n    return 8900c1044b314c736b175bafe6c9d2884d5352f1\nAuthor: Gianluca Bocanegra <gianlutenis@gmail.com>\nDate:   Tue Sep 29 21:47:38 2026 -0400\n\n    T04: Events and the Menu on the public site\n    \n    Events get an Event date (required start date, optional start time and end\n    date), validated in the console and rejected on News and Menu Posts. Export\n    renders Upcoming Events (soonest first) and past Events (most recent first)\n    in eventos/, shows the next 3 Upcoming Events and only the Current menu on\n    the Home page, and lists every other Published Menu in menus/, newest first.\n    Older databases gain the Event date columns on startup.\n    \n    Closes #5\n    \n    Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5.5 {"parentUuid":"c954c1c6-65dc-4ef1-8b29-a67ed21836bf","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfhUnRXZ8xwtePqsYyEpQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GF7oT2w6PA3DQFVEFs6rJw","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"python3 - <<'EOF'\np='tests/test_t03_publish_export.py'\ns=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace('fields = dict(re.findall(r\\'name=\"([^\"]+)\" value=\"([^\"]*)\"\\', form.group(2)))',\n 'fields = {k: html.unescape(v) for k, v in\\n              re.findall(r\\'name=\"([^\"]+)\" value=\"([^\"]*)\"\\', form.group(2))}')\ns=s.replace('import re\\n','import html\\nimport re\\n',1)\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncat > tests/test_t05_confirmation.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"T05: Confirmation screens. Seam 1, the console over HTTP.\"\"\"\nimport re\nfrom datetime import date\n\nfrom app.publish import render_site\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_from\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import save_post\nfrom tests.test_t03_publish_export import confirm, new_post, post_action, publish\n\nPRICE_MENU = \"Lomo saltado S/ 35\"\n\n\ndef ask(client, location, action, **fields):\n    \"\"\"The first submission of an action, with the editor's current values.\"\"\"\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(location))\n    return client.post(f\"{location}/{action}\", data={\"csrf_token\": token, **fields},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef editor_values(**over):\n    return {\"title\": \"Menú\", \"slug\": \"menu\", \"body_md\": PRICE_MENU, **over}\n\n\ndef status_of(client, location):\n    return \"Published\" if \"Status: Published\" in client.get(location).text else \"Draft\"\n\n\ndef is_live(tmp_path, name):\n    return (render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", export_date=date(2026, 9, 29)) / name).exists()\n\n\n# --- publish -------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_publish_shows_a_confirmation_with_the_preview_and_changes_nothing(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Menú\", slug=\"menu\", body=PRICE_MENU)\n    screen = ask(editor, location, \"publish\", **editor_values())\n    assert screen.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Confirm\" in screen.text and PRICE_MENU in screen.text\n    assert status_of(editor, location) == \"Draft\"\n\n\ndef test_confirmed_publish_applies_the_values_on_the_screen(client_as, tmp_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Menú\", slug=\"menu\", body=PRICE_MENU)\n    screen = ask(editor, location, \"publish\", **editor_values(body_md=\"Lomo saltado S/ 38\"))\n    assert \"S/ 38\" in screen.text\n    assert confirm(editor, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert status_of(editor, location) == \"Published\"\n    export = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", export_date=date(2026, 9, 29))\n    assert \"S/ 38\" in (export / \"menus\" / \"menu.html\").read_text()\n\n\ndef test_publish_with_an_invalid_value_shows_the_form_error_not_a_confirmation(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Menú\")\n    response = ask(editor, location, \"publish\", **editor_values(title=\"\"))\n    assert response.status_code == 422 and \"title is required\" in response.text\n    assert status_of(editor, location) == \"Draft\"\n\n\n# --- saving edits to Published content -----------------------------------\n\ndef test_saving_edits_to_a_published_post_needs_confirmation(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = publish(editor, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Menú\", slug=\"menu\", body=PRICE_MENU)\n    screen = ask(editor, location, \"\", **editor_values(body_md=\"Lomo saltado S/ 99\"))\n    assert screen.status_code == 200 and \"S/ 99\" in screen.text\n    assert PRICE_MENU in editor.get(location).text  # stored Post unchanged\n    assert confirm(editor, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert \"S/ 99\" in editor.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_saving_a_draft_needs_no_confirmation(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Menú\", slug=\"menu\", body=PRICE_MENU)\n    response = ask(editor, location, \"\", **editor_values(body_md=\"Nuevo\"))\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert \"Nuevo\" in editor.get(location).text\n\n\n# --- unpublish and delete ------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_unpublish_needs_confirmation(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    screen = ask(editor, location, \"unpublish\")\n    assert screen.status_code == 200\n    assert status_of(editor, location) == \"Published\"\n    assert confirm(editor, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert status_of(editor, location) == \"Draft\"\n\n\ndef test_delete_needs_confirmation_that_says_it_is_permanent(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    screen = ask(editor, location, \"delete\")\n    assert screen.status_code == 200 and \"permanent\" in screen.text\n    assert editor.get(location).status_code == 200  # still there\n    assert confirm(editor, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert editor.get(location).status_code == 404\n    assert \"Torneo\" not in editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n\n\ndef test_deleting_a_published_post_removes_it_from_the_site_at_next_export(client_as, tmp_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    assert is_live(tmp_path, \"noticias/torneo.html\")\n    confirm(editor, ask(editor, location, \"delete\"))\n    assert not is_live(tmp_path, \"noticias/torneo.html\")\n\n\n# --- cancel --------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_cancel_returns_to_the_editor_with_the_unsaved_values(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = publish(editor, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Menú\", slug=\"menu\", body=PRICE_MENU)\n    screen = ask(editor, location, \"\", **editor_values(title=\"Menú nuevo\",\n                                                       body_md='Sin \"comillas\" <b>'))\n    cancel = re.search(r'formaction=\"([^\"]+)\"[^>]*value=\"cancel\"', screen.text)\n    assert cancel, \"no cancel button\"\n    fields = dict(re.findall(r'name=\"([^\"]+)\" value=\"([^\"]*)\"', screen.text))\n    back = editor.post(cancel.group(1), data=fields, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert back.status_code == 200\n    assert 'value=\"Menú nuevo\"' in back.text and \"Sin &#34;comillas&#34; &lt;b&gt;\" in back.text\n    assert PRICE_MENU in editor.get(location).text  # nothing was saved\n\n\n# --- CSRF ----------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_confirmed_submission_without_a_csrf_token_is_rejected(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    response = editor.post(f\"{location}/publish\", data={\"confirm\": \"1\", **editor_values()})\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n    assert status_of(editor, location) == \"Draft\"\n    assert editor.post(f\"{location}/delete\", data={\"confirm\": \"1\"}).status_code == 403\n    assert editor.get(location).status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_every_confirmation_form_carries_the_csrf_token(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    for action in (\"unpublish\", \"delete\"):\n        csrf_from(ask(editor, location, action))\n\n\n# --- not yet Live --------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef not_live(client):\n    return int(re.search(r\"Changes not yet Live: (\\d+)\", client.get(\"/admin\").text).group(1))\n\n\ndef test_deleting_a_live_post_counts_as_not_yet_live_until_the_next_export(client_as, tmp_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    is_live(tmp_path, \"index.html\")  # an Export\n    assert not_live(editor) == 0\n    confirm(editor, ask(editor, location, \"delete\"))\n    assert not_live(editor) == 1\n    is_live(tmp_path, \"index.html\")\n    assert not_live(editor) == 0\n\n\ndef test_published_work_counts_as_not_yet_live_until_exported(client_as, tmp_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert not_live(editor) == 0\n    publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    assert not_live(editor) == 1\n    is_live(tmp_path, \"index.html\")\n    assert not_live(editor) == 0\nEOF\nuv run pytest -q tests/test_t05_confirmation.py 2>&1 | tail -20","description":"Write T05 tests and run them client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = publish(editor, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Menú\", slug=\"menu\", body=PRICE_MENU)\n    screen = ask(editor, location, \"\", **editor_values(title=\"Menú nuevo\",\n                                                       body_md='Sin \"comillas\" <b>'))\n    cancel = re.search(r'formaction=\"([^\"]+)\"[^>]*value=\"cancel\"', screen.text)\n    assert cancel, \"no cancel button\"\n    fields = dict(re.findall(r'name=\"([^\"]+)\" value=\"([^\"]*)\"', screen.text))\n    back = editor.post(cancel.group(1), data=fields, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert back.status_code == 200\n    assert 'value=\"Menú nuevo\"' in back.text and \"Sin &#34;comillas&#34; &lt;b&gt;\" in back.text\n    assert PRICE_MENU in editor.get(location).text  # nothing was saved\n\n\n# --- CSRF ----------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_confirmed_submission_without_a_csrf_token_is_rejected(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    response = editor.post(f\"{location}/publish\", data={\"confirm\": \"1\", **editor_values()})\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n    assert status_of(editor, location) == \"Draft\"\n    assert editor.post(f\"{location}/delete\", data={\"confirm\": \"1\"}).status_code == 403\n    assert editor.get(location).status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_every_confirmation_form_carries_the_csrf_token(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    for action in (\"unpublish\", \"delete\"):\n        csrf_from(ask(editor, location, action))\n\n\n# --- not yet Live --------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef not_live(client):\n    return int(re.search(r\"Changes not yet Live: (\\d+)\", client.get(\"/admin\").text).group(1))\n\n\ndef test_deleting_a_live_post_counts_as_not_yet_live_until_the_next_export(client_as, tmp_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    is_live(tmp_path, \"index.html\")  # an Export\n    assert not_live(editor) == 0\n    confirm(editor, ask(editor, location, \"delete\"))\n    assert not_live(editor) == 1\n    is_live(tmp_path, \"index.html\")\n    assert not_live(editor) == 0\n\n\ndef test_published_work_counts_as_not_yet_live_until_exported(client_as, tmp_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert not_live(editor) == 0\n    publish(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    assert not_live(editor) == 1\n    is_live(tmp_path, \"index.html\")\n    assert not_live(editor) == 0\nEOF\nuv run pytest -q tests/test_t05_confirmation.py 2>&1 | tail -20","description":"Write T05 tests and run them Counted as a\n-- change not yet Live until the next Export.\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS deleted_published (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    deleted_at TEXT NOT NULL\n);\n''')\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\n\n# content\np='app/content.py'\ns=open(p).read()\nold=s[s.index('def update_post('):s.index('def get_post(')]\nnew='''def clean_update(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str,\n                 slug: str, event_start_date: str = \"\", event_start_time: str = \"\",\n                 event_end_date: str = \"\") -> dict:\n    \"\"\"The editable values as they would be stored, or a PostError. Changes nothing.\n\n    Kind and Author never change. The Slug is locked once first published.\n    \"\"\"\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    event = _clean_event(post[\"kind\"], event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date)\n    if post[\"first_published_at\"]:\n        slug = post[\"slug\"]\n    else:\n        slug = _slug_for(conn, post[\"kind\"], title, slug or post[\"slug\"], post[\"id\"])\n    return {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"event_start_date\": event[0],\n            \"event_start_time\": event[1], \"event_end_date\": event[2]}\n\n\ndef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str,\n                slug: str, body_md: str, event_start_date: str = \"\",\n                event_start_time: str = \"\", event_end_date: str = \"\") -> None:\n    v = clean_update(conn, post, title=title, slug=slug, event_start_date=event_start_date,\n                     event_start_time=event_start_time, event_end_date=event_end_date)\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, slug = ?, body_md = ?, updated_at = ?, \"\n                 \"event_start_date = ?, event_start_time = ?, event_end_date = ? \"\n                 \"WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (v[\"title\"], v[\"slug\"], body_md, utc_now(), v[\"event_start_date\"],\n                  v[\"event_start_time\"], v[\"event_end_date\"], post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\n'''\ns=s.replace(old,new)\ns=s.replace('''def published_posts(''','''def delete_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Permanently remove a Post. One that was ever Published is remembered, so\n    the not-yet-Live count includes it until the next Export.\"\"\"\n    if post[\"first_published_at\"]:\n        conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO deleted_published (deleted_at) VALUES (?)\", (utc_now(),))\n    conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post[\"id\"],))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef changes_not_live(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Changes Members can't see yet: Posts touched since the last Export, plus\n    Live Posts deleted since then. Before any Export, every Published Post counts.\"\"\"\n    last = conn.execute(\"SELECT max(ran_at) FROM exports\").fetchone()[0]\n    if last is None:\n        return conn.execute(\"SELECT count(*) FROM posts WHERE status = 'published'\"\n                            ).fetchone()[0]\n    touched = conn.execute(\"SELECT count(*) FROM posts WHERE updated_at > ?\",\n                           (last,)).fetchone()[0]\n    deleted = conn.execute(\"SELECT count(*) FROM deleted_published WHERE deleted_at > ?\",\n                           (last,)).fetchone()[0]\n    return touched + deleted\n\n\ndef published_posts(''',1)\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF","description":"Add delete, clean_update, not-yet-Live count, schema table"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4250,"cache_read_input_tokens":77234,"output_tokens":1775,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":108},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4250,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1775,"cache_read_input_tokens":77234,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4250,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4250},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard","fallback_credit":null},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01NBMqaU4ieD1na1ExU98QFv":{"command":"cd /home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms && python3 - Changes nothing.\n\n    Kind and Author never change. The Slug is locked once first published.\n    \"\"\"\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    event = _clean_event(post[\"kind\"], event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date)\n    if post[\"first_published_at\"]:\n        slug = post[\"slug\"]\n    else:\n        slug = _slug_for(conn, post[\"kind\"], title, slug or post[\"slug\"], post[\"id\"])\n    return {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"event_start_date\": event[0],\n            \"event_start_time\": event[1], \"event_end_date\": event[2]}\n\n\ndef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str,\n                slug: str, body_md: str, event_start_date: str = \"\",\n                event_start_time: str = \"\", event_end_date: str = \"\") -> None:\n    v = clean_update(conn, post, title=title, slug=slug, event_start_date=event_start_date,\n                     event_start_time=event_start_time, event_end_date=event_end_date)\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, slug = ?, body_md = ?, updated_at = ?, \"\n                 \"event_start_date = ?, event_start_time = ?, event_end_date = ? \"\n                 \"WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (v[\"title\"], v[\"slug\"], body_md, utc_now(), v[\"event_start_date\"],\n                  v[\"event_start_time\"], v[\"event_end_date\"], post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\n'''\ns=s.replace(old,new)\ns=s.replace('''def published_posts(''','''def delete_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Permanently remove a Post. One that was ever Published is remembered, so\n    the not-yet-Live count includes it until the next Export.\"\"\"\n    if post[\"first_published_at\"]:\n        conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO deleted_published (deleted_at) VALUES (?)\", (utc_now(),))\n    conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post[\"id\"],))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef changes_not_live(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Changes Members can't see yet: Posts touched since the last Export, plus\n    Live Posts deleted since then. Before any Export, every Published Post counts.\"\"\"\n    last = conn.execute(\"SELECT max(ran_at) FROM exports\").fetchone()[0]\n    if last is None:\n        return conn.execute(\"SELECT count(*) FROM posts WHERE status = 'published'\"\n                            ).fetchone()[0]\n    touched = conn.execute(\"SELECT count(*) FROM posts WHERE updated_at > ?\",\n                           (last,)).fetchone()[0]\n    deleted = conn.execute(\"SELECT count(*) FROM deleted_published WHERE deleted_at > ?\",\n                           (last,)).fetchone()[0]\n    return touched + deleted\n\n\ndef published_posts(''',1)\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF","description":"Add delete, clean_update, not-yet-Live count, schema table"}},"wireIngestContext":{"toolu_01NBMqaU4ieD1na1ExU98QFv":{"cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms"}},"serverClassifierRequest":"8033f1de-b8ee-49bb-8ad2-ca3e6c4d0569","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfhUpVMYgo36YSJffXB28","attributionSkill":"implement","type":"assistant","uuid":"c9322ea3-a154-4017-9cfd-bd6c6916a40c","timestamp":"2026-10-04T16:04:10.918Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"c9432616-ab73-4985-a1c5-6391db0fa022","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"e31a54a4-37c7-4d6f-a759-1926c25809ff","version":"2.1.285","gitBranch":"main"}

“{post[key] or ''}” → “{cleaned[key] or ''}”\"\n             for key, label in labels.items() if (post[key] or \"\") != (cleaned[key] or \"\")]\n    if post[\"body_md\"] != body_md:\n        lines.append(\"Body: changed (see the preview below)\")\n    return lines or [\"No changes.\"]\n\n\ndef confirmation(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, post: sqlite3.Row, *, action: str,\n carries the pending values in hidden fields. Only a second POST with the\n    confirm flag applies the change; Cancel reopens the editor with the values.\n    \"\"\"\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/confirm.html\",\n        {\"title\": heading, \"user\": user, \"post\": post, \"summary\": summary,\n         \"pending\": pending or {}, \"action\": action, \"button\": button,\n         \"preview\": preview and article_context(preview)})\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef post_list(''',1)\n\n# save_post .. unpublish\nstart=s.index('@router.post(\"/{post_id}\")')\nend=s.index('@router.get(\"/{post_id}/preview\")')\nnew='''def editor_page(request, user, post: sqlite3.Row, values: dict | None, error: str,\n                status_code: int = 422):\n    return form_page(request, user, {**dict(post), **(values or {})}, error=error,\n                     status_code=status_code)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef save_post(request: Request, post_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n              values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    values = values or {k: \"\" for k in EDITOR_FIELDS}\n    try:\n        cleaned = content.clean_update(conn, post, **{k: v for k, v in values.items()\n                                                      if k != \"body_md\"})\n        if post[\"status\"] == \"published\" and not confirm:\n            return confirmation(\n                request, user, post, action=\"save_post\", button=\"Save changes\",\n                heading=\"Confirm changes to Published post\", pending=values,\n                summary=[\"This post is Published. It stays a Draft until you confirm, \"\n                         \"and reaches the public site at the next Export.\"])\n        if values is not None:\n            content.update_post(conn, post, **values)\n            post = load(conn, post_id)\n    except content.PostError as exc:\n        return editor_page(request, user, post, values, str(exc))\n    content.publish_post(conn, post)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/unpublish\")\ndef unpublish_post(request: Request, post_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n                   values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n                   user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                   conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    if not confirm:\n        return confirmation(\n            request, user, post, action=\"unpublish_post\", button=\"Unpublish\",\n            heading=\"Confirm unpublishing\", pending=values,\n            summary=[f\"Unpublish “{post['title']}”? It returns to Draft and disappears \"\n                     \"from the public site at the next Export. Unsaved edits in the \"\n                     \"editor are not saved.\"])\n    content.unpublish_post(conn, post)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/delete\")\ndef delete_post(request: Request, post_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n                values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n                user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    if not confirm:\n        return confirmation(\n            request, user, post, action=\"delete_post\", button=\"Delete permanently\",\n            heading=\"Confirm deleting\", pending=values,\n            summary=[f\"Delete “{post['title']}”? {"parentUuid":"b4ca47de-1798-4779-98c4-59ee770093c8","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"96303632-9da0-49ca-b24e-0e1d26baa801","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/.claude/skills/code-review\n\nTwo-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:\n\n- **Standards**: does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?\n- **Spec**: does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / spec?\n\nBoth axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.\n\nThe issue tracker should have been provided to you. If `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md` is missing, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Pin the fixed point\n\nWhatever the user said is the fixed point (a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc.). If they didn't specify one, ask for it.\n\nCapture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.\n\nBefore going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here, not inside two parallel sub-agents.\n\n### 2. Identify the spec source\n\nLook for the originating spec, in this order:\n\n1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.), fetched via the workflow in `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\n2. A path the user passed as an argument.\n3. A spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.\n4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report \"no spec available\".\n\n### 3. Identify the standards sources\n\nAnything in the repo that documents how code should be written, such as `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md`.\n\nOn top of whatever the repo documents, the Standards axis always carries the **smell baseline** below: a fixed set of Fowler code smells (_Refactoring_, ch.3) that applies even when a repo documents nothing. Two rules bind it:\n\n- **The repo overrides.** A documented repo standard always wins; where it endorses something the baseline would flag, suppress the smell.\n- **Always a judgement call.** Each smell is a labelled heuristic (\"possible Feature Envy\"), never a hard violation. Like any standard here, skip anything tooling already enforces.\n\nEach smell reads *what it is* → *how to fix*; match it against the diff:\n\n- **Mysterious Name**: a function, variable, or type whose name doesn't reveal what it does or holds. → rename it; if no honest name comes, the design's murky.\n- **Duplicated Code**: the same logic shape appears in more than one hunk or file in the change. → extract the shared shape, call it from both.\n- **Feature Envy**: a method that reaches into another object's data more than its own. → move the method onto the data it envies.\n- **Data Clumps**: the same few fields or params keep travelling together (a type wanting to be born). → bundle them into one type, pass that.\n- **Primitive Obsession**: a primitive or string standing in for a domain concept that deserves its own type. → give the concept its own small type.\n- **Repeated Switches**: the same `switch`/`if`-cascade on the same type recurs across the change. → replace with polymorphism, or one map both sites share.\n- **Shotgun Surgery**: one logical change forces scattered edits across many files in the diff. → gather what changes together into one module.\n- **Divergent Change**: one file or module is edited for several unrelated reasons. → split so each module changes for one reason.\n- **Speculative Generality**: abstraction, parameters, or hooks added for needs the spec doesn't have. → delete it; inline back until a real need shows.\n- **Message Chains**: long `a.b().c().d()` navigation the caller shouldn't depend on. → hide the walk behind one method on the first object.\n- **Middle Man**: a class or function that mostly just delegates onward. → cut it, call the real target direct.\n- **Refused Bequest**: a subclass or implementer that ignores or overrides most of what it inherits. → drop the inheritance, use composition.\n\n### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel\n\n**Standards sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The full diff command and commit list.\n- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3, **plus the smell baseline from step 3** pasted in full (the sub-agent has no other access to it).\n- The brief: \"Report, per file/hunk where relevant, (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls: documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words.\"\n\n**Spec sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The diff command and commit list.\n- The path or fetched contents of the spec.\n- The brief: \"Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words.\"\n\nIf the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.\n\n### 5. Aggregate\n\nPresent the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings, because the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).\n\nEnd with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes: that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.\n\n## Why two axes\n\nA change can pass one axis and fail the other:\n\n- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing → **Standards pass, Spec fail.**\n- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions → **Spec pass, Standards fail.**\n\nReporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.\n\n\nARGUMENTS: since HEAD (uncommitted T05 changes), ticket #6, spec #1"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"8cb4d33f-9016-4967-a802-96fba273a881","timestamp":"2026-10-04T16:06:38.488Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_0196DPURdSkqhFx9bem8U9P8","session_id":"c9432616-ab73-4985-a1c5-6391db0fa022","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"e31a54a4-37c7-4d6f-a759-1926c25809ff","version":"2.1.285","gitBranch":"main"}

It is not published output, so it is only a risk if preview output ever reaches `site/`. Verify that it doesn't.\n  2. HARD (needs checking). Every state-changing form carries a CSRF token. `templates/admin/post_form.html` now has the Publish, Unpublish and Delete buttons inside the main form. The test `test_every_confirmation_form_carries_the_csrf_token` covers only the unpublish and delete confirmation screens. The editor form needs to include `csrf_token` for these buttons to work, and the diff doesn't show that.\n  3. HARD. Tests go through the HTTP seam and render_site output, never table queries (the Testing Decisions rule). I found no table queries in the tests. `changes_not_live` is tested through the dashboard text, which complies.\n  4. Judgement. CONTEXT.md vocabulary:\n     - The code and UI use \"Changes not yet Live\", which is consistent with **Live**.\n     - `Confirmation` is used correctly.\n     - `deleted_published` is not a glossary term, but it is internal, so this is minor.\n     - The `post.html` template has a stray unindented `{% include %}`. This is cosmetic.\n  \n  I found no violations of the hard constraints on secrets, argon2, Markdown sanitisation, `site/` generation or drafts. `_article.html` still uses `body_html | safe`. That is acceptable only because `render_markdown` sanitises its output. In the confirm screen the preview body comes from unsaved user input through the same `render_markdown`, so it is sanitised.\n  \n  **(b) Baseline smells**\n  \n  - Duplicated Code (judgement). The same two lines appear in `save_post` and `publish_post`:\n    `content.clean_update(conn, post, **{k: v for k, v in values.items() if k != \"body_md\"})`\n    The `values.items() ... != \"body_md\"` filter is a Data Clump. Extract a helper, or have `clean_update` accept `body_md`.\n  - Duplicated Code and Repeated Switches (judgement). `confirmation()` is called from four routes. Each one repeats the same unconfirmed-then-confirmed `if not confirm:` gate. The Publish and Unpublish switch is also repeated in the `post_form.html` template and in the routes.\n  - Primitive Obsession (judgement). Pending values travel as an untyped `dict` with string keys. `EDITOR_FIELDS`, the `labels` dict in `changes()` and the `update_post` signature all list the same fields. That is Shotgun Surgery risk when a field is added.\n  - Mysterious Name (judgement):\n    - `path_for_list` is a Middle Man. It is a one-line wrapper called from one place.\n    - `editor_page` and `form_page` overlap.\n    - `shown` in `publish_post` is vague.\n    - `clean_update` returns a dict, and `update_post` then unpacks it field by field.\n  - Feature Envy (judgement). `article_context` lives in the routes module but only builds data from the post fields. It belongs near the rendering code, and `render_site` probably duplicates this context.\n  - Complexity in `publish_post` (judgement). It has `values is not None` checked three times, with a conditional dict merge. Normalise `values` to a dict early.\n  - Speculative Generality (judgement). `status_code=422` is a parameter on `editor_page` that nobody overrides. `confirmation(... preview: dict | None)` calls `article_context(preview)` inside the function, which hides a transformation.\n  - Test duplication (judgement). `confirm()` and the hidden-field regexes are duplicated between the T03 and T05 tests. `ask()` has an over-long line.\n</agent-message>"}
